import type { ReactNode } from 'react'

type Props = {
  label: string
  error?: string
  children: ReactNode
}

export function FormField({ label, error, children }: Props) {
  return (
    <div className="space-y-2">
      <label className="text-sm font-medium">{label}</label>

      {children}

      {error && <p className="text-xs text-destructive">{error}</p>}
    </div>
  )
}


############################################################
FILE: src/components/kesehatan/pemeriksaan-form.tsx
############################################################

import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod'
import {
  Activity,
  ClipboardCheck,
  FlaskConical,
  Ruler,
  UsersRound,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { useEffect } from 'react'
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form'
import type { UseFormRegisterReturn } from 'react-hook-form'
import { toast } from 'sonner'

import { FormField } from '@/components/kesehatan/form-field'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea'

import {
  savePemeriksaanKesehatan,
  updatePemeriksaanKesehatan,
} from '@/server/kesehatan'

import { pemeriksaanKesehatanFieldsSchema } from '@/server/kesehatan/schemas'
import type { PemeriksaanKesehatanFormValues } from '@/server/kesehatan/schemas'

type PetugasEventItem = {
  id: number
  nama: string
}

type PemeriksaanAwal = Omit<
  PemeriksaanKesehatanFormValues,
  'keluhan' | 'hasilRingkas'
> & {
  id: number
  keluhan: string | null
  hasilRingkas: string | null
}

type Props = {
  eventId: number
  anggotaId: number

  petugas: PetugasEventItem[]

  initialValues?: PemeriksaanAwal

  onSaved: () => void | Promise<void>
  onCancel?: () => void
}

const emptyValues: PemeriksaanKesehatanFormValues = {
  tensiSistolik: null,
  tensiDiastolik: null,

  gulaDarah: null,
  kolesterol: null,
  asamUrat: null,

  beratBadan: null,
  tinggiBadan: null,

  keluhan: '',
  hasilRingkas: '',
}

function getFormValues(
  initialValues?: PemeriksaanAwal,
): PemeriksaanKesehatanFormValues {
  if (!initialValues) {
    return emptyValues
  }

  return {
    tensiSistolik: initialValues.tensiSistolik,
    tensiDiastolik: initialValues.tensiDiastolik,

    gulaDarah: initialValues.gulaDarah,
    kolesterol: initialValues.kolesterol,
    asamUrat: initialValues.asamUrat,

    beratBadan: initialValues.beratBadan,
    tinggiBadan: initialValues.tinggiBadan,

    keluhan: initialValues.keluhan ?? '',
    hasilRingkas: initialValues.hasilRingkas ?? '',
  }
}

const numberInputOptions = {
  setValueAs: (value: unknown) => {
    if (value === null || value === undefined || value === '') {
      return null
    }

    const number = Number(value)

    return Number.isNaN(number) ? value : number
  },
}

export function PemeriksaanForm({
  eventId,
  anggotaId,
  petugas,
  initialValues,
  onSaved,
  onCancel,
}: Props) {
  const form = useForm<PemeriksaanKesehatanFormValues>({
    resolver: zodResolver(pemeriksaanKesehatanFieldsSchema),

    defaultValues: getFormValues(initialValues),
  })

  const {
    register,
    handleSubmit,
    reset,

    formState: { errors, isSubmitting },
  } = form

  useEffect(() => {
    reset(getFormValues(initialValues))
  }, [initialValues, reset])

  async function onSubmit(values: PemeriksaanKesehatanFormValues) {
    try {
      if (initialValues) {
        await updatePemeriksaanKesehatan({
          data: {
            id: initialValues.id,

            ...values,
          },
        })

        toast.success('Hasil pemeriksaan berhasil diperbarui.')
      } else {
        await savePemeriksaanKesehatan({
          data: {
            eventId,
            anggotaId,

            ...values,
          },
        })

        toast.success('Hasil pemeriksaan berhasil disimpan.')
      }

      await onSaved()
    } catch (error) {
      toast.error('Gagal menyimpan pemeriksaan', {
        description:
          error instanceof Error
            ? error.message
            : 'Terjadi kesalahan pada server.',
      })
    }
  }

  return (
    <form className="space-y-6" onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)}>
      {/* TANDA VITAL */}
      <section>
        <SectionTitle
          icon={<Activity className="size-4" />}
          title="Tanda Vital"
        />

        <div className="mt-3">
          <FormField
            label="Tekanan Darah"
            error={
              errors.tensiSistolik?.message ?? errors.tensiDiastolik?.message
            }
          >
            <div className="grid grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_auto_minmax(0,1fr)_auto] items-center gap-2">
              <Input
                type="number"
                inputMode="numeric"
                placeholder="120"
                disabled={isSubmitting}
                className="h-10 rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white text-center shadow-none"
                {...register('tensiSistolik', numberInputOptions)}
              />

              <span className="text-sm text-slate-400">/</span>

              <Input
                type="number"
                inputMode="numeric"
                placeholder="80"
                disabled={isSubmitting}
                className="h-10 rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white text-center shadow-none"
                {...register('tensiDiastolik', numberInputOptions)}
              />

              <span className="whitespace-nowrap text-xs text-slate-400">
                mmHg
              </span>
            </div>
          </FormField>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* PEMERIKSAAN */}
      <section className="border-t border-slate-100 pt-5">
        <SectionTitle
          icon={<FlaskConical className="size-4" />}
          title="Pemeriksaan"
        />

        <div className="mt-3 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
          <NumberField
            label="Gula Darah"
            suffix="mg/dL"
            disabled={isSubmitting}
            error={errors.gulaDarah?.message}
            registration={register('gulaDarah', numberInputOptions)}
          />

          <NumberField
            label="Kolesterol"
            suffix="mg/dL"
            disabled={isSubmitting}
            error={errors.kolesterol?.message}
            registration={register('kolesterol', numberInputOptions)}
          />

          <NumberField
            label="Asam Urat"
            suffix="mg/dL"
            step="0.1"
            disabled={isSubmitting}
            error={errors.asamUrat?.message}
            registration={register('asamUrat', numberInputOptions)}
          />
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* ANTROPOMETRI */}
      <section className="border-t border-slate-100 pt-5">
        <SectionTitle
          icon={<Ruler className="size-4" />}
          title="Berat & Tinggi Badan"
        />

        <div className="mt-3 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
          <NumberField
            label="Berat Badan"
            suffix="kg"
            step="0.1"
            disabled={isSubmitting}
            error={errors.beratBadan?.message}
            registration={register('beratBadan', numberInputOptions)}
          />

          <NumberField
            label="Tinggi Badan"
            suffix="cm"
            step="0.1"
            disabled={isSubmitting}
            error={errors.tinggiBadan?.message}
            registration={register('tinggiBadan', numberInputOptions)}
          />
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* CATATAN */}
      <section className="border-t border-slate-100 pt-5">
        <SectionTitle
          icon={<ClipboardCheck className="size-4" />}
          title="Catatan Pemeriksaan"
        />

        <div className="mt-3 grid gap-4 xl:grid-cols-2">
          <FormField label="Keluhan" error={errors.keluhan?.message}>
            <Textarea
              {...register('keluhan')}
              disabled={isSubmitting}
              placeholder="Keluhan warga..."
              className="min-h-24 resize-y rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-none"
            />
          </FormField>

          <FormField label="Hasil Ringkas" error={errors.hasilRingkas?.message}>
            <Textarea
              {...register('hasilRingkas')}
              disabled={isSubmitting}
              placeholder="Catatan atau hasil pemeriksaan..."
              className="min-h-24 resize-y rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-none"
            />
          </FormField>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* PETUGAS */}
      <section className="border-t border-slate-100 pt-5">
        <div className="flex items-start gap-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/70 px-4 py-3.5">
          <div className="flex size-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-white text-slate-500 ring-1 ring-slate-200">
            <UsersRound className="size-4" />
          </div>

          <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
            <div className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">
              Petugas Event
            </div>

            {petugas.length > 0 ? (
              <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
                {petugas.map((item) => item.nama).join(', ')}
              </p>
            ) : (
              <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-red-600">
                Belum ada petugas yang didaftarkan.
              </p>
            )}
          </div>

          {petugas.length > 0 && (
            <span className="shrink-0 rounded-md bg-white px-2 py-1 text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-500 ring-1 ring-slate-200">
              {petugas.length}
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* ACTION */}
      <div className="flex flex-col-reverse gap-2 border-t border-slate-100 pt-5 sm:flex-row sm:justify-end">
        {onCancel && (
          <Button
            type="button"
            variant="outline"
            className="h-10 rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white sm:min-w-24"
            disabled={isSubmitting}
            onClick={onCancel}
          >
            Batal
          </Button>
        )}

        <Button
          type="submit"
          className="h-10 rounded-lg sm:min-w-44"
          disabled={isSubmitting || petugas.length === 0}
        >
          {isSubmitting
            ? 'Menyimpan...'
            : initialValues
              ? 'Simpan Perubahan'
              : 'Simpan Pemeriksaan'}
        </Button>
      </div>
    </form>
  )
}

type SectionTitleProps = {
  icon: React.ReactNode
  title: string
}

function SectionTitle({ icon, title }: SectionTitleProps) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">
      <span className="text-slate-400">{icon}</span>

      {title}
    </div>
  )
}

type NumberFieldProps = {
  label: string
  suffix: string
  step?: string
  disabled?: boolean
  error?: string

  registration: UseFormRegisterReturn
}

function NumberField({
  label,
  suffix,
  step,
  disabled,
  error,
  registration,
}: NumberFieldProps) {
  return (
    <FormField label={label} error={error}>
      <div className="flex h-10 overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white focus-within:border-ring focus-within:ring-3 focus-within:ring-ring/50">
        <Input
          type="number"
          step={step}
          inputMode="decimal"
          disabled={disabled}
          {...registration}
          className="h-full min-w-0 flex-1 rounded-none border-0 bg-transparent shadow-none focus-visible:ring-0"
        />

        <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center border-l border-slate-100 bg-slate-50 px-3 text-xs text-slate-500">
          {suffix}
        </div>
      </div>
    </FormField>
  )
}


############################################################
FILE: src/components/kesehatan/petugas-event.tsx
############################################################

import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod'
import { Plus, Trash2, UsersRound } from 'lucide-react'

import { useState } from 'react'
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form'
import { toast } from 'sonner'

import { FormField } from '@/components/kesehatan/form-field'

import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'

import { hapusPetugasEvent, tambahPetugasEvent } from '@/server/kesehatan'

import { petugasEventFormSchema } from '@/server/kesehatan/schemas'

import type { PetugasEventFormValues } from '@/server/kesehatan/schemas'

type Petugas = {
  id: number
  nama: string
}

type Props = {
  eventId: number
  petugas: Petugas[]

  onChanged: () => void | Promise<void>
}

export function PetugasEvent({ eventId, petugas, onChanged }: Props) {
  const [deletingId, setDeletingId] = useState<number | null>(null)

  const form = useForm<PetugasEventFormValues>({
    resolver: zodResolver(petugasEventFormSchema),

    defaultValues: {
      nama: '',
    },
  })

  const {
    register,
    reset,
    setError,

    formState: { errors, isSubmitting },
  } = form

  async function onSubmit(values: PetugasEventFormValues) {
    const exists = petugas.some(
      (item) => item.nama.toLowerCase() === values.nama.trim().toLowerCase(),
    )

    if (exists) {
      setError('nama', {
        type: 'manual',
        message: 'Petugas tersebut sudah ditambahkan.',
      })

      return
    }

    try {
      await tambahPetugasEvent({
        data: {
          eventId,
          nama: values.nama,
        },
      })

      toast.success('Petugas berhasil ditambahkan.')

      reset({
        nama: '',
      })

      await onChanged()
    } catch (error) {
      toast.error('Gagal menambahkan petugas', {
        description:
          error instanceof Error
            ? error.message
            : 'Terjadi kesalahan pada server.',
      })
    }
  }

  async function handleDelete(item: Petugas) {
    const confirmed = window.confirm(
      `Hapus ${item.nama} dari daftar petugas event?`,
    )

    if (!confirmed) {
      return
    }

    setDeletingId(item.id)

    try {
      await hapusPetugasEvent({
        data: {
          id: item.id,
        },
      })

      toast.success('Petugas berhasil dihapus.')

      await onChanged()
    } catch (error) {
      toast.error('Gagal menghapus petugas', {
        description:
          error instanceof Error
            ? error.message
            : 'Terjadi kesalahan pada server.',
      })
    } finally {
      setDeletingId(null)
    }
  }

  const isBusy = isSubmitting || deletingId !== null

  return (
    <Card className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
      <CardHeader className="border-b border-slate-200 px-5 py-4 sm:px-6">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
          <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-3">
            <div className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
              <UsersRound className="size-4" />
            </div>

            <div className="min-w-0">
              <CardTitle className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">
                Petugas Event
              </CardTitle>

              <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500">
                Kelola petugas yang terlibat dalam kegiatan ini.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <span className="shrink-0 rounded-md bg-slate-100 px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-500">
            {petugas.length.toLocaleString('id-ID')} petugas
          </span>
        </div>
      </CardHeader>

      <CardContent className="p-5 sm:p-6">
        <form
          className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_auto] sm:items-end"
          onSubmit={form.handleSubmit(onSubmit)}
        >
          <FormField label="Tambah Petugas" error={errors.nama?.message}>
            <Input
              {...register('nama')}
              disabled={isBusy}
              placeholder="Masukkan nama petugas"
              autoComplete="off"
              className="h-10 rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-none"
            />
          </FormField>

          <Button
            type="submit"
            variant="outline"
            disabled={isBusy}
            className="h-10 rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white px-4 shadow-none"
          >
            <Plus className="size-4" />

            {isSubmitting ? 'Menambahkan...' : 'Tambah'}
          </Button>
        </form>

        <div className="mt-5 border-t border-slate-100 pt-5">
          {petugas.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/50 px-5 py-8 text-center">
              <div className="mx-auto flex size-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-white text-slate-400 ring-1 ring-slate-200">
                <UsersRound className="size-4" />
              </div>

              <div className="mt-3 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">
                Belum ada petugas
              </div>

              <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
                Tambahkan petugas yang bertanggung jawab pada event kesehatan
                ini.
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              {petugas.map((item) => {
                const isDeleting = deletingId === item.id

                return (
                  <div
                    key={item.id}
                    className="inline-flex max-w-full items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-slate-700"
                  >
                    <span className="truncate font-medium">{item.nama}</span>

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      disabled={isBusy}
                      onClick={() => void handleDelete(item)}
                      className="flex size-6 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-md text-slate-400 transition hover:bg-white hover:text-red-600 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
                      aria-label={`Hapus ${item.nama}`}
                      title={`Hapus ${item.nama}`}
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="size-3.5" />
                    </button>

                    {isDeleting && (
                      <span className="sr-only">Menghapus...</span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                )
              })}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  )
}

FILE: src/components/kesehatan/ringkasan-kesehatan.tsx
############################################################

import {
  Activity,
  CalendarDays,
  Droplets,
  HeartPulse,
  Ruler,
  Scale,
  TestTube2,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import type { ReactNode } from 'react'

import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card'

import { getRingkasanKesehatan } from '@/server/kesehatan'

type Ringkasan = Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getRingkasanKesehatan>>

type Props = {
  anggotaId: number
}

export function RingkasanKesehatan({ anggotaId }: Props) {
  const [data, setData] = useState<Ringkasan | null>(null)

  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true)

  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false

    async function load() {
      setIsLoading(true)
      setError(null)

      try {
        const result = await getRingkasanKesehatan({
          data: {
            anggotaId,
          },
        })

        if (!cancelled) {
          setData(result)
        }
      } catch (error) {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setError(
            error instanceof Error
              ? error.message
              : 'Gagal memuat ringkasan kesehatan.',
          )
        }
      } finally {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setIsLoading(false)
        }
      }
    }

    void load()

    return () => {
      cancelled = true
    }
  }, [anggotaId])

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <Card className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
        <CardHeader className="border-b border-slate-200 px-5 py-4 sm:px-6">
          <RingkasanHeader />
        </CardHeader>

        <CardContent className="p-5 sm:p-6">
          <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
            {Array.from({
              length: 8,
            }).map((_, index) => (
              <div
                key={index}
                className="animate-pulse rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/60 p-4"
              >
                <div className="h-3 w-20 rounded bg-slate-200" />
                <div className="mt-3 h-5 w-24 rounded bg-slate-200" />
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    )
  }

  if (error) {
    return (
      <Card className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border-red-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
        <CardHeader className="border-b border-red-100 px-5 py-4 sm:px-6">
          <RingkasanHeader />
        </CardHeader>

        <CardContent className="p-5 sm:p-6">
          <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50/60 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">
            {error}
          </div>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    )
  }

  if (!data || !data.terakhir) {
    return (
      <Card className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
        <CardHeader className="border-b border-slate-200 px-5 py-4 sm:px-6">
          <RingkasanHeader />
        </CardHeader>

        <CardContent className="p-5 sm:p-6">
          <div className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/50 px-6 py-10 text-center">
            <div className="mx-auto flex size-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-white text-slate-400 ring-1 ring-slate-200">
              <HeartPulse className="size-4" />
            </div>

            <div className="mt-3 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">
              Belum ada data kesehatan
            </div>

            <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
              Ringkasan kesehatan akan muncul setelah anggota memiliki data
              pemeriksaan.
            </p>
          </div>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    )
  }

  const terakhir = data.terakhir

  return (
    <Card className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
      <CardHeader className="border-b border-slate-200 px-5 py-4 sm:px-6">
        <RingkasanHeader />
      </CardHeader>

      <CardContent className="p-5 sm:p-6">
        <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
          <Metric
            icon={<Activity className="size-4" />}
            label="Pemeriksaan"
            value={`${data.jumlahPemeriksaan.toLocaleString('id-ID')} kali`}
          />

          <Metric
            icon={<CalendarDays className="size-4" />}
            label="Terakhir"
            value={formatTanggal(terakhir.tanggal)}
          />

          <Metric
            icon={<HeartPulse className="size-4" />}
            label="Tekanan Darah"
            value={formatTensi(terakhir.tensiSistolik, terakhir.tensiDiastolik)}
          />

          <Metric
            icon={<Droplets className="size-4" />}
            label="Gula Darah"
            value={formatNilai(terakhir.gulaDarah, 'mg/dL')}
          />

          <Metric
            icon={<TestTube2 className="size-4" />}
            label="Kolesterol"
            value={formatNilai(terakhir.kolesterol, 'mg/dL')}
          />

          <Metric
            icon={<TestTube2 className="size-4" />}
            label="Asam Urat"
            value={formatNilai(terakhir.asamUrat, 'mg/dL')}
          />

          <Metric
            icon={<Scale className="size-4" />}
            label="Berat Badan"
            value={formatNilai(terakhir.beratBadan, 'kg')}
          />

          <Metric
            icon={<Ruler className="size-4" />}
            label="Tinggi Badan"
            value={formatNilai(terakhir.tinggiBadan, 'cm')}
          />
        </div>

        <div className="mt-5 grid gap-4 border-t border-slate-100 pt-5 lg:grid-cols-2">
          <div className="rounded-xl bg-slate-50 px-4 py-3.5">
            <div className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-400">
              Pemeriksaan Terakhir
            </div>

            <div className="mt-1 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">
              {terakhir.eventNama}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-xl bg-slate-50 px-4 py-3.5">
            <div className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-400">
              Tanggal Pemeriksaan
            </div>

            <div className="mt-1 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">
              {formatTanggal(terakhir.tanggal)}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {terakhir.hasilRingkas && (
          <div className="mt-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-4 py-3.5">
            <div className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-400">
              Hasil Ringkas Terakhir
            </div>

            <p className="mt-1.5 whitespace-pre-wrap text-sm leading-6 text-slate-700">
              {terakhir.hasilRingkas}
            </p>
          </div>
        )}
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  )
}

function RingkasanHeader() {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
      <div className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
        <HeartPulse className="size-4" />
      </div>

      <div>
        <CardTitle className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">
          Ringkasan Kesehatan
        </CardTitle>

        <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
          Kondisi berdasarkan pemeriksaan kesehatan terakhir
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function Metric({
  icon,
  label,
  value,
}: {
  icon: ReactNode
  label: string
  value: string
}) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/60 p-4">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-medium text-slate-400">
        {icon}
        {label}
      </div>

      <div className="mt-2 text-base font-semibold leading-6 text-slate-900">
        {value}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function formatTensi(sistolik: number | null, diastolik: number | null) {
  if (sistolik === null && diastolik === null) {
    return '-'
  }

  return `${sistolik ?? '-'}/${diastolik ?? '-'} mmHg`
}

function formatNilai(value: number | null, suffix: string) {
  if (value === null) {
    return '-'
  }

  return `${value.toLocaleString('id-ID')} ${suffix}`
}

function formatTanggal(value: string) {
  const [year, month, day] = value.split('-').map(Number)

  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('id-ID', {
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'long',
    year: 'numeric',
  }).format(new Date(year, month - 1, day))
}

FILE: src/components/kesehatan/riwayat-pemeriksaan.tsx
############################################################

import {
  Activity,
  CalendarDays,
  ClipboardList,
  History,
  UsersRound,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'

import { getRiwayatPemeriksaan } from '@/server/kesehatan'

type Riwayat = Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getRiwayatPemeriksaan>>[number]

type Props = {
  anggotaId: number
  refreshKey?: number
}

export function RiwayatPemeriksaan({ anggotaId, refreshKey = 0 }: Props) {
  const [data, setData] = useState<Riwayat[]>([])

  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true)

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false

    async function load() {
      setIsLoading(true)

      try {
        const result = await getRiwayatPemeriksaan({
          data: {
            anggotaId,
          },
        })

        if (!cancelled) {
          setData(result)
        }
      } finally {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setIsLoading(false)
        }
      }
    }

    void load()

    return () => {
      cancelled = true
    }
  }, [anggotaId, refreshKey])

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="space-y-3">
        <RiwayatHeader count={null} />

        <div className="space-y-3">
          {Array.from({
            length: 2,
          }).map((_, index) => (
            <div
              key={index}
              className="animate-pulse rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4"
            >
              <div className="h-4 w-40 rounded bg-slate-100" />

              <div className="mt-2 h-3 w-28 rounded bg-slate-100" />

              <div className="mt-5 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
                {Array.from({
                  length: 6,
                }).map((__, itemIndex) => (
                  <div key={itemIndex}>
                    <div className="h-3 w-16 rounded bg-slate-100" />

                    <div className="mt-2 h-4 w-20 rounded bg-slate-100" />
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  if (data.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="space-y-3">
        <RiwayatHeader count={0} />

        <div className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/50 px-6 py-10 text-center">
          <div className="mx-auto flex size-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-white text-slate-400 ring-1 ring-slate-200">
            <History className="size-4" />
          </div>

          <div className="mt-3 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">
            Belum ada riwayat kesehatan
          </div>

          <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
            Riwayat pemeriksaan warga akan muncul di sini setelah pemeriksaan
            tersimpan.
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      <RiwayatHeader count={data.length} />

      <div className="space-y-3">
        {data.map((item, index) => (
          <article
            key={item.id}
            className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white"
          >
            {/* EVENT */}
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 border-b border-slate-100 bg-slate-50/60 px-4 py-3.5 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                  <h3 className="truncate text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">
                    {item.event.nama}
                  </h3>

                  {index === 0 && (
                    <span className="rounded-full bg-blue-50 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold text-blue-600">
                      Terbaru
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>

                <div className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-slate-500">
                  <CalendarDays className="size-3.5" />

                  {formatTanggal(item.event.tanggal)}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-slate-500">
                <UsersRound className="size-3.5 shrink-0" />

                <span className="truncate">
                  {item.event.petugas.length > 0
                    ? item.event.petugas
                        .map((petugas) => petugas.nama)
                        .join(', ')
                    : 'Petugas tidak tercatat'}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* NILAI PEMERIKSAAN */}
            <div className="p-4">
              <div className="grid gap-x-5 gap-y-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
                <Item
                  label="Tekanan Darah"
                  value={
                    item.tensiSistolik !== null && item.tensiDiastolik !== null
                      ? `${item.tensiSistolik}/${item.tensiDiastolik}`
                      : '-'
                  }
                  suffix={
                    item.tensiSistolik !== null && item.tensiDiastolik !== null
                      ? 'mmHg'
                      : undefined
                  }
                />

                <Item
                  label="Gula Darah"
                  value={item.gulaDarah !== null ? String(item.gulaDarah) : '-'}
                  suffix={item.gulaDarah !== null ? 'mg/dL' : undefined}
                />

                <Item
                  label="Kolesterol"
                  value={
                    item.kolesterol !== null ? String(item.kolesterol) : '-'
                  }
                  suffix={item.kolesterol !== null ? 'mg/dL' : undefined}
                />

                <Item
                  label="Asam Urat"
                  value={item.asamUrat !== null ? String(item.asamUrat) : '-'}
                  suffix={item.asamUrat !== null ? 'mg/dL' : undefined}
                />

                <Item
                  label="Berat Badan"
                  value={
                    item.beratBadan !== null ? String(item.beratBadan) : '-'
                  }
                  suffix={item.beratBadan !== null ? 'kg' : undefined}
                />

                <Item
                  label="Tinggi Badan"
                  value={
                    item.tinggiBadan !== null ? String(item.tinggiBadan) : '-'
                  }
                  suffix={item.tinggiBadan !== null ? 'cm' : undefined}
                />
              </div>

              {(item.keluhan || item.hasilRingkas) && (
                <div className="mt-4 grid gap-3 border-t border-slate-100 pt-4 lg:grid-cols-2">
                  {item.keluhan && (
                    <CatatanItem label="Keluhan" value={item.keluhan} />
                  )}

                  {item.hasilRingkas && (
                    <CatatanItem
                      label="Hasil Ringkas"
                      value={item.hasilRingkas}
                    />
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </article>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function RiwayatHeader({ count }: { count: number | null }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <History className="size-4 text-slate-400" />

        <div>
          <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">
            Riwayat Kesehatan
          </div>

          <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500">
            Pemeriksaan kesehatan warga dari event sebelumnya.
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {count !== null && (
        <span className="shrink-0 rounded-md bg-slate-100 px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-500">
          {count.toLocaleString('id-ID')} pemeriksaan
        </span>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

function Item({
  label,
  value,
  suffix,
}: {
  label: string
  value: string
  suffix?: string
}) {
  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-400">
        <Activity className="size-3" />

        {label}
      </div>

      <div className="mt-1 flex items-baseline gap-1">
        <span className="text-sm font-semibold tabular-nums text-slate-900">
          {value}
        </span>

        {suffix && <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">{suffix}</span>}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function CatatanItem({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-lg bg-slate-50 px-3.5 py-3">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">
        <ClipboardList className="size-3.5" />
        {label}
      </div>

      <p className="mt-1.5 whitespace-pre-wrap text-sm leading-6 text-slate-700">
        {value}
      </p>
    </div>
  )
}

function formatTanggal(value: string) {
  const [year, month, day] = value.split('-').map(Number)

  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('id-ID', {
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'long',
    year: 'numeric',
  }).format(new Date(year, month - 1, day))
}


############################################################
FILE: src/components/kesehatan/selected-warga-kesehatan.tsx
############################################################

import { CheckCircle2, ClipboardPlus, History, UserRound } from 'lucide-react'
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'

import { PemeriksaanForm } from '@/components/kesehatan/pemeriksaan-form'
import { RiwayatPemeriksaan } from '@/components/kesehatan/riwayat-pemeriksaan'

import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card'

import type { WargaKesehatan } from '@/components/kesehatan/use-warga-kesehatan-search'

type PetugasEventItem = {
  id: number
  nama: string
}

type Tab = 'pemeriksaan' | 'riwayat'

type Props = {
  eventId: number
  warga: WargaKesehatan
  petugas: PetugasEventItem[]

  refreshKey: number

  onSaved: () => void | Promise<void>
}

export function SelectedWargaKesehatan({
  eventId,
  warga,
  petugas,
  refreshKey,
  onSaved,
}: Props) {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<Tab>('pemeriksaan')

  useEffect(() => {
    setActiveTab('pemeriksaan')
  }, [warga.id])

  async function handleSaved() {
    await onSaved()

    setActiveTab('riwayat')
  }

  return (
    <Card className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
      <CardHeader className="border-b border-slate-200 px-5 py-4 sm:px-6">
        <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
          <div className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
            <UserRound className="size-4" />
          </div>

          <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
            <div className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-blue-600">
              Warga Dipilih
            </div>

            <CardTitle className="mt-1 text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">
              {warga.nama}
            </CardTitle>

            <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500">NIK {warga.nik}</p>
          </div>

          {warga.sudahDiperiksa && (
            <span className="inline-flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-500">
              <CheckCircle2 className="size-3.5" />
              Sudah diperiksa
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
      </CardHeader>

      {/* TAB */}
      <div className="border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/50 px-4 pt-3 sm:px-5">
        <div className="flex gap-1">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('pemeriksaan')}
            className={[
              'flex h-10 items-center gap-2 rounded-t-lg border-b-2 px-3 text-sm font-medium transition',
              activeTab === 'pemeriksaan'
                ? 'border-blue-600 bg-white text-blue-700'
                : 'border-transparent text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800',
            ].join(' ')}
          >
            <ClipboardPlus className="size-4" />
            Pemeriksaan Baru
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('riwayat')}
            className={[
              'flex h-10 items-center gap-2 rounded-t-lg border-b-2 px-3 text-sm font-medium transition',
              activeTab === 'riwayat'
                ? 'border-blue-600 bg-white text-blue-700'
                : 'border-transparent text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800',
            ].join(' ')}
          >
            <History className="size-4" />
            Riwayat Kesehatan
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <CardContent className="p-5 sm:p-6">
        {activeTab === 'pemeriksaan' ? (
          warga.sudahDiperiksa ? (
            <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 px-4 py-4">
              <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                <div className="flex size-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-white text-slate-500 ring-1 ring-slate-200">
                  <CheckCircle2 className="size-4" />
                </div>

                <div className="min-w-0">
                  <div className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">
                    Pemeriksaan event ini sudah tersimpan
                  </div>

                  <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
                    Data pemeriksaan warga ini sudah dicatat. Gunakan tab
                    Riwayat Kesehatan untuk melihat hasil pemeriksaan.
                  </p>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setActiveTab('riwayat')}
                    className="mt-3 text-xs font-semibold text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-700"
                  >
                    Lihat riwayat kesehatan
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <PemeriksaanForm
              eventId={eventId}
              anggotaId={warga.id}
              petugas={petugas}
              onSaved={handleSaved}
            />
          )
        ) : (
          <RiwayatPemeriksaan anggotaId={warga.id} refreshKey={refreshKey} />
        )}
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  )
}


############################################################
FILE: src/components/kesehatan/tren-kesehatan.tsx
############################################################

import { Activity, Droplets, HeartPulse, Scale, TestTube2 } from 'lucide-react'
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import type { ReactNode } from 'react'

import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card'

import { getTrenKesehatan } from '@/server/kesehatan'

type TrenData = Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getTrenKesehatan>>

type TrenItem = TrenData[number]

type Metric = 'tensi' | 'gulaDarah' | 'kolesterol' | 'asamUrat' | 'beratBadan'

type Props = {
  anggotaId: number
}

export function TrenKesehatan({ anggotaId }: Props) {
  const [data, setData] = useState<TrenData>([])

  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true)

  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)

  const [metric, setMetric] = useState<Metric>('tensi')

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false

    async function load() {
      setIsLoading(true)
      setError(null)

      try {
        const result = await getTrenKesehatan({
          data: {
            anggotaId,
          },
        })

        if (!cancelled) {
          setData(result)
        }
      } catch (error) {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setError(
            error instanceof Error
              ? error.message
              : 'Gagal memuat tren kesehatan.',
          )
        }
      } finally {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setIsLoading(false)
        }
      }
    }

    void load()

    return () => {
      cancelled = true
    }
  }, [anggotaId])

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <Card className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
        <CardHeader className="border-b border-slate-200 px-5 py-4 sm:px-6">
          <TrenHeader />
        </CardHeader>

        <CardContent className="p-5 sm:p-6">
          <div className="animate-pulse">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              {Array.from({
                length: 5,
              }).map((_, index) => (
                <div key={index} className="h-9 w-24 rounded-lg bg-slate-100" />
              ))}
            </div>

            <div className="mt-5 h-72 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50" />
          </div>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    )
  }

  if (error) {
    return (
      <Card className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border-red-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
        <CardHeader className="border-b border-red-100 px-5 py-4 sm:px-6">
          <TrenHeader />
        </CardHeader>

        <CardContent className="p-5 sm:p-6">
          <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50/60 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">
            {error}
          </div>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    )
  }

  if (data.length === 0) {
    return (
      <Card className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
        <CardHeader className="border-b border-slate-200 px-5 py-4 sm:px-6">
          <TrenHeader />
        </CardHeader>

        <CardContent className="p-5 sm:p-6">
          <div className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/50 px-6 py-10 text-center">
            <div className="mx-auto flex size-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-white text-slate-400 ring-1 ring-slate-200">
              <Activity className="size-4" />
            </div>

            <div className="mt-3 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">
              Belum ada tren kesehatan
            </div>

            <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
              Grafik perkembangan akan muncul setelah tersedia data pemeriksaan
              kesehatan.
            </p>
          </div>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    )
  }

  const visibleData = data.length > 12 ? data.slice(-12) : data

  return (
    <Card className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
      <CardHeader className="border-b border-slate-200 px-5 py-4 sm:px-6">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-start sm:justify-between">
          <TrenHeader />

          <span className="w-fit shrink-0 rounded-md bg-slate-100 px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-500">
            {data.length.toLocaleString('id-ID')} pemeriksaan
          </span>
        </div>
      </CardHeader>

      <CardContent className="p-5 sm:p-6">
        <MetricSelector value={metric} onChange={setMetric} />

        <div className="mt-5">
          <ChartForMetric metric={metric} data={visibleData} />
        </div>

        {data.length > 12 && (
          <p className="mt-4 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-400">
            Grafik menampilkan 12 pemeriksaan terakhir agar perkembangan tetap
            mudah dibaca.
          </p>
        )}
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  )
}

function TrenHeader() {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
      <div className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
        <Activity className="size-4" />
      </div>

      <div>
        <CardTitle className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">
          Tren Kesehatan
        </CardTitle>

        <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
          Perkembangan hasil pemeriksaan dari waktu ke waktu
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function MetricSelector({
  value,
  onChange,
}: {
  value: Metric
  onChange: (value: Metric) => void
}) {
  const options: Array<{
    value: Metric
    label: string
  }> = [
    {
      value: 'tensi',
      label: 'Tensi',
    },
    {
      value: 'gulaDarah',
      label: 'Gula Darah',
    },
    {
      value: 'kolesterol',
      label: 'Kolesterol',
    },
    {
      value: 'asamUrat',
      label: 'Asam Urat',
    },
    {
      value: 'beratBadan',
      label: 'Berat Badan',
    },
  ]

  return (
    <div className="overflow-x-auto">
      <div className="flex min-w-max gap-1 rounded-xl bg-slate-100/80 p-1">
        {options.map((option) => {
          const active = value === option.value

          return (
            <button
              key={option.value}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onChange(option.value)}
              className={[
                'h-9 rounded-lg px-3 text-sm font-medium transition',
                active
                  ? 'bg-white text-blue-700 shadow-sm ring-1 ring-slate-200'
                  : 'text-slate-500 hover:bg-white/60 hover:text-slate-800',
              ].join(' ')}
            >
              {option.label}
            </button>
          )
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function ChartForMetric({
  metric,
  data,
}: {
  metric: Metric
  data: TrenItem[]
}) {
  if (metric === 'tensi') {
    return (
      <div className="space-y-3">
        <ChartHeading
          icon={<HeartPulse className="size-4" />}
          title="Tekanan Darah"
        />

        <TrendChart
          data={data}
          unit="mmHg"
          series={[
            {
              label: 'Sistolik',
              values: data.map((item) => item.tensiSistolik),
              stroke: 'var(--primary)',
            },
            {
              label: 'Diastolik',
              values: data.map((item) => item.tensiDiastolik),
              stroke: 'var(--muted-foreground)',
            },
          ]}
        />
      </div>
    )
  }

  if (metric === 'gulaDarah') {
    return (
      <MetricChart
        icon={<Droplets className="size-4" />}
        title="Gula Darah"
        unit="mg/dL"
        data={data}
        values={data.map((item) => item.gulaDarah)}
      />
    )
  }

  if (metric === 'kolesterol') {
    return (
      <MetricChart
        icon={<TestTube2 className="size-4" />}
        title="Kolesterol"
        unit="mg/dL"
        data={data}
        values={data.map((item) => item.kolesterol)}
      />
    )
  }

  if (metric === 'asamUrat') {
    return (
      <MetricChart
        icon={<TestTube2 className="size-4" />}
        title="Asam Urat"
        unit="mg/dL"
        data={data}
        values={data.map((item) => item.asamUrat)}
      />
    )
  }

  return (
    <MetricChart
      icon={<Scale className="size-4" />}
      title="Berat Badan"
      unit="kg"
      data={data}
      values={data.map((item) => item.beratBadan)}
    />
  )
}

function MetricChart({
  icon,
  title,
  unit,
  data,
  values,
}: {
  icon: ReactNode
  title: string
  unit: string
  data: TrenItem[]
  values: Array<number | null>
}) {
  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      <ChartHeading icon={icon} title={title} />

      <TrendChart
        data={data}
        unit={unit}
        series={[
          {
            label: title,
            values,
            stroke: 'var(--primary)',
          },
        ]}
      />
    </div>
  )
}

function ChartHeading({ icon, title }: { icon: ReactNode; title: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
      <span className="text-slate-400">{icon}</span>

      <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">{title}</h3>
    </div>
  )
}

type Series = {
  label: string
  values: Array<number | null>
  stroke: string
}

function TrendChart({
  data,
  series,
  unit,
}: {
  data: TrenItem[]
  series: Series[]
  unit: string
}) {
  const allValues = series
    .flatMap((item) => item.values)
    .filter((value): value is number => value !== null)

  if (allValues.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/50 py-12 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">
        Belum ada data untuk grafik ini.
      </div>
    )
  }

  const width = 760
  const height = 280

  const paddingLeft = 58
  const paddingRight = 24
  const paddingTop = 20
  const paddingBottom = 46

  const chartWidth = width - paddingLeft - paddingRight

  const chartHeight = height - paddingTop - paddingBottom

  const rawMin = Math.min(...allValues)

  const rawMax = Math.max(...allValues)

  const rawRange = rawMax - rawMin

  const padding =
    rawRange === 0 ? Math.max(Math.abs(rawMax) * 0.1, 1) : rawRange * 0.12

  const minValue = Math.max(0, rawMin - padding)

  const maxValue = rawMax + padding

  const range = maxValue - minValue || 1

  function getX(index: number) {
    if (data.length <= 1) {
      return paddingLeft + chartWidth / 2
    }

    return paddingLeft + (index / (data.length - 1)) * chartWidth
  }

  function getY(value: number) {
    return paddingTop + ((maxValue - value) / range) * chartHeight
  }

  const tickCount = 4

  const yTicks = Array.from(
    {
      length: tickCount,
    },
    (_, index) => {
      const ratio = index / (tickCount - 1)

      return maxValue - ratio * (maxValue - minValue)
    },
  )

  const xLabelStep = data.length <= 6 ? 1 : Math.ceil(data.length / 6)

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white">
        <svg
          viewBox={`0 0 ${width} ${height}`}
          className="min-w-[620px] w-full"
          role="img"
          aria-label={`Grafik ${series.map((item) => item.label).join(', ')}`}
        >
          {yTicks.map((value, index) => {
            const y = paddingTop + (index / (tickCount - 1)) * chartHeight

            return (
              <g key={`${value}-${index}`}>
                <line
                  x1={paddingLeft}
                  x2={width - paddingRight}
                  y1={y}
                  y2={y}
                  stroke="var(--border)"
                  strokeWidth="1"
                />

                <text
                  x={paddingLeft - 8}
                  y={y + 4}
                  textAnchor="end"
                  fontSize="11"
                  fill="var(--muted-foreground)"
                >
                  {formatAxisValue(value)}
                </text>
              </g>
            )
          })}

          {data.map((item, index) => {
            if (index % xLabelStep !== 0 && index !== data.length - 1) {
              return null
            }

            return (
              <text
                key={item.id}
                x={getX(index)}
                y={height - 16}
                textAnchor="middle"
                fontSize="10"
                fill="var(--muted-foreground)"
              >
                {formatShortDate(item.tanggal)}
              </text>
            )
          })}

          {series.map((item) => {
            const segments = buildSegments(item.values, getX, getY)

            return (
              <g key={item.label}>
                {segments.map((points, index) => (
                  <polyline
                    key={index}
                    points={points}
                    fill="none"
                    stroke={item.stroke}
                    strokeWidth="2.5"
                    strokeLinecap="round"
                    strokeLinejoin="round"
                    vectorEffect="non-scaling-stroke"
                  />
                ))}

                {item.values.map((value, index) => {
                  if (value === null) {
                    return null
                  }

                  return (
                    <circle
                      key={index}
                      cx={getX(index)}
                      cy={getY(value)}
                      r="4"
                      fill={item.stroke}
                      stroke="white"
                      strokeWidth="2"
                      vectorEffect="non-scaling-stroke"
                    >
                      <title>
                        {`${item.label}: ${formatDisplayValue(
                          value,
                        )} ${unit} — ${formatTanggal(data[index].tanggal)} — ${
                          data[index].eventNama
                        }`}
                      </title>
                    </circle>
                  )
                })}
              </g>
            )
          })}
        </svg>
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4 text-xs text-slate-500">
        {series.map((item) => (
          <div key={item.label} className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span
              className="h-0.5 w-5 rounded-full"
              style={{
                backgroundColor: item.stroke,
              }}
            />

            {item.label}
          </div>
        ))}

        <span className="ml-auto">Satuan: {unit}</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function buildSegments(
  values: Array<number | null>,
  getX: (index: number) => number,
  getY: (value: number) => number,
) {
  const segments: string[] = []

  let current: string[] = []

  values.forEach((value, index) => {
    if (value === null) {
      if (current.length > 0) {
        segments.push(current.join(' '))

        current = []
      }

      return
    }

    current.push(`${getX(index)},${getY(value)}`)
  })

  if (current.length > 0) {
    segments.push(current.join(' '))
  }

  return segments
}

function formatAxisValue(value: number) {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat('id-ID', {
    maximumFractionDigits: 1,
  }).format(value)
}

function formatDisplayValue(value: number) {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat('id-ID', {
    maximumFractionDigits: 1,
  }).format(value)
}

function formatShortDate(value: string) {
  const [year, month, day] = value.split('-').map(Number)

  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('id-ID', {
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'short',
  }).format(new Date(year, month - 1, day))
}

function formatTanggal(value: string) {
  const [year, month, day] = value.split('-').map(Number)

  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('id-ID', {
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'long',
    year: 'numeric',
  }).format(new Date(year, month - 1, day))
}

FILE: src/components/kesehatan/use-pemeriksaan-event.ts
############################################################

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'

import { listPemeriksaanEvent } from '@/server/kesehatan'

export type PemeriksaanEventItem = Awaited<
  ReturnType<typeof listPemeriksaanEvent>
>[number]

type Props = {
  eventId: number
  refreshKey: number
}

export function usePemeriksaanEvent({ eventId, refreshKey }: Props) {
  const [data, setData] = useState<PemeriksaanEventItem[]>([])

  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true)

  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false

    async function load() {
      setIsLoading(true)
      setError(null)

      try {
        const result = await listPemeriksaanEvent({
          data: {
            eventId,
          },
        })

        if (!cancelled) {
          setData(result)
        }
      } catch (error) {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setError(
            error instanceof Error
              ? error.message
              : 'Gagal memuat pemeriksaan.',
          )
        }
      } finally {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setIsLoading(false)
        }
      }
    }

    void load()

    return () => {
      cancelled = true
    }
  }, [eventId, refreshKey])

  return {
    data,
    isLoading,
    error,
  }
}


############################################################
FILE: src/components/kesehatan/use-warga-kesehatan-search.ts
############################################################

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'

import { searchWargaKesehatan } from '@/server/kesehatan'

export type WargaKesehatan = Awaited<
  ReturnType<typeof searchWargaKesehatan>
>[number]

type Props = {
  eventId: number
  refreshKey: number
}

export function useWargaKesehatanSearch({ eventId, refreshKey }: Props) {
  const [q, setQ] = useState('')
  const [results, setResults] = useState<WargaKesehatan[]>([])

  const [isSearching, setIsSearching] = useState(false)
  const [hasSearched, setHasSearched] = useState(false)

  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    const query = q.trim()

    setHasSearched(false)
    setError(null)

    if (!query) {
      setResults([])
      setIsSearching(false)

      return
    }

    let cancelled = false

    const timer = window.setTimeout(async () => {
      setIsSearching(true)

      try {
        const data = await searchWargaKesehatan({
          data: {
            eventId,
            q: query,
          },
        })

        if (!cancelled) {
          setResults(data)
          setHasSearched(true)
        }
      } catch (error) {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setResults([])
          setHasSearched(true)

          setError(
            error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Gagal mencari warga.',
          )
        }
      } finally {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setIsSearching(false)
        }
      }
    }, 350)

    return () => {
      cancelled = true
      window.clearTimeout(timer)
    }
  }, [q, eventId, refreshKey])

  function markAsExamined(anggotaId: number) {
    setResults((current) =>
      current.map((item) =>
        item.id === anggotaId
          ? {
              ...item,
              sudahDiperiksa: true,
            }
          : item,
      ),
    )
  }

  return {
    q,
    setQ,

    results,

    isSearching,
    hasSearched,
    error,

    markAsExamined,
  }
}

FILE: src/components/kesehatan/warga-kesehatan-section.tsx

import {
  ArrowLeft,
  CheckCircle2,
  LoaderCircle,
  MapPin,
  Search,
  SearchX,
  UserRound,
  UsersRound,
  X,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { useState } from 'react'

import { SelectedWargaKesehatan } from '@/components/kesehatan/selected-warga-kesehatan'
import { useWargaKesehatanSearch } from '@/components/kesehatan/use-warga-kesehatan-search'

import type { WargaKesehatan } from '@/components/kesehatan/use-warga-kesehatan-search'

import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'

type PetugasEventItem = {
  id: number
  nama: string
}

type Props = {
  eventId: number
  petugas: PetugasEventItem[]
  refreshKey: number
  onChanged: () => void | Promise<void>
}

const MAX_VISIBLE_RESULTS = 10

export function WargaKesehatanSection({
  eventId,
  petugas,
  refreshKey,
  onChanged,
}: Props) {
  const { q, setQ, results, isSearching, hasSearched, error, markAsExamined } =
    useWargaKesehatanSearch({
      eventId,
      refreshKey,
    })

  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<WargaKesehatan | null>(null)

  async function handleSaved() {
    if (!selected) {
      return
    }

    const anggotaId = selected.id

    setSelected((current) =>
      current
        ? {
            ...current,
            sudahDiperiksa: true,
          }
        : current,
    )

    markAsExamined(anggotaId)

    await onChanged()
  }

  function handleSelect(warga: WargaKesehatan) {
    if (warga.sudahDiperiksa) {
      return
    }

    setSelected(warga)
  }

  function handleChangeWarga() {
    setSelected(null)
  }

  const query = q.trim()

  const showEmpty =
    query.length > 0 &&
    hasSearched &&
    !isSearching &&
    results.length === 0 &&
    !error

  const visibleResults = results.slice(0, MAX_VISIBLE_RESULTS)

  return (
    <section className="space-y-4">
      {/* SEARCH
          Di mobile disembunyikan setelah warga dipilih.
          Di desktop selalu tersedia. */}
      <Card
        className={[
          'overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm',
          selected ? 'hidden lg:block' : '',
        ].join(' ')}
      >
        <CardHeader className="border-b border-slate-200 px-5 py-4 sm:px-6">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
              <Search className="size-4" />
            </div>

            <div className="min-w-0">
              <CardTitle className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">
                Cari Warga
              </CardTitle>

              <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500">
                Cari berdasarkan nama atau NIK lengkap.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </CardHeader>

        <CardContent className="p-5 sm:p-6">
          <div className="relative">
            <Search className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 size-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />

            <Input
              value={q}
              autoComplete="off"
              placeholder="Ketik nama atau NIK..."
              className="h-11 rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white pl-9 pr-11 shadow-none"
              onChange={(event) => setQ(event.target.value)}
            />

            <div className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2">
              {isSearching ? (
                <LoaderCircle className="size-4 animate-spin text-slate-400" />
              ) : q ? (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setQ('')}
                  className="flex size-6 items-center justify-center rounded-md text-slate-400 transition hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-700"
                  aria-label="Hapus pencarian"
                >
                  <X className="size-4" />
                </button>
              ) : null}
            </div>
          </div>

          <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-400">
            Untuk pencarian NIK, masukkan nomor secara lengkap.
          </p>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      {error && (
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50/60 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* MOBILE: tombol kembali ke daftar warga */}
      {selected && (
        <div className="lg:hidden">
          <Button
            type="button"
            variant="outline"
            className="h-10 rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white"
            onClick={handleChangeWarga}
          >
            <ArrowLeft className="size-4" />
            Ganti warga
          </Button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* WORKSPACE */}
      <div className="grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-[340px_minmax(0,1fr)] lg:items-start">
        {/* MASTER: HASIL PENCARIAN */}
        <div className={selected ? 'hidden lg:block' : 'block'}>
          {visibleResults.length > 0 ? (
            <Card className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm lg:sticky lg:top-24">
              <CardHeader className="border-b border-slate-200 px-4 py-4">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                  <div>
                    <CardTitle className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">
                      Hasil Pencarian
                    </CardTitle>

                    <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500">
                      Pilih warga yang akan diperiksa.
                    </p>
                  </div>

                  <span className="shrink-0 rounded-md bg-slate-100 px-2 py-1 text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-500">
                    {results.length.toLocaleString('id-ID')} hasil
                  </span>
                </div>
              </CardHeader>

              <CardContent className="p-0">
                <div className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
                  {visibleResults.map((warga) => {
                    const isSelected = selected?.id === warga.id

                    const isDisabled = warga.sudahDiperiksa

                    return (
                      <button
                        key={warga.id}
                        type="button"
                        disabled={isDisabled}
                        onClick={() => handleSelect(warga)}
                        className={[
                          'flex w-full items-start gap-3 px-4 py-3.5 text-left transition-colors',
                          isDisabled
                            ? 'cursor-not-allowed bg-slate-50/80 opacity-65'
                            : isSelected
                              ? 'bg-blue-50/70'
                              : 'hover:bg-slate-50',
                        ].join(' ')}
                      >
                        <div
                          className={[
                            'mt-0.5 flex size-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg',
                            isSelected
                              ? 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-600'
                              : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-500',
                          ].join(' ')}
                        >
                          <UserRound className="size-4" />
                        </div>

                        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                          <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                            <span className="truncate text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">
                              {warga.nama}
                            </span>

                            {isDisabled && (
                              <CheckCircle2 className="mt-0.5 size-4 shrink-0 text-slate-400" />
                            )}
                          </div>

                          <div className="mt-1 truncate text-xs text-slate-500">
                            NIK {warga.nik}
                          </div>

                          {(warga.alamat || warga.desaKelurahan) && (
                            <div className="mt-1.5 flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 text-xs text-slate-400">
                              <MapPin className="size-3 shrink-0" />

                              <span className="truncate">
                                {[warga.alamat, warga.desaKelurahan]
                                  .filter(Boolean)
                                  .join(', ')}
                              </span>
                            </div>
                          )}

                          {isDisabled && (
                            <div className="mt-2 text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-400">
                              Sudah diperiksa
                            </div>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      </button>
                    )
                  })}
                </div>

                {results.length > MAX_VISIBLE_RESULTS && (
                  <div className="border-t border-slate-100 bg-slate-50/60 px-4 py-3 text-center text-xs text-slate-500">
                    Menampilkan {MAX_VISIBLE_RESULTS} dari{' '}
                    {results.length.toLocaleString('id-ID')} hasil. Persempit
                    pencarian untuk menemukan warga lainnya.
                  </div>
                )}
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          ) : showEmpty ? (
            <div className="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-slate-200 bg-white px-5 py-10 text-center">
              <div className="mx-auto flex size-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-slate-100 text-slate-400">
                <SearchX className="size-4" />
              </div>

              <div className="mt-3 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">
                Warga tidak ditemukan
              </div>

              <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
                Periksa kembali nama atau NIK yang dimasukkan.
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="hidden rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-slate-200 bg-white px-5 py-10 text-center lg:block">
              <div className="mx-auto flex size-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-slate-100 text-slate-400">
                <UsersRound className="size-4" />
              </div>

              <div className="mt-3 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">
                Belum ada warga dipilih
              </div>

              <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
                Gunakan pencarian di atas untuk menemukan warga.
              </p>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* DETAIL: FORM PEMERIKSAAN */}
        <div className={selected ? 'block' : 'hidden lg:block'}>
          {selected ? (
            <SelectedWargaKesehatan
              eventId={eventId}
              warga={selected}
              petugas={petugas}
              refreshKey={refreshKey}
              onSaved={handleSaved}
            />
          ) : (
            <div className="flex min-h-72 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-slate-200 bg-white px-6 py-12 text-center">
              <div>
                <div className="mx-auto flex size-12 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-500">
                  <UserRound className="size-5" />
                </div>

                <div className="mt-4 font-semibold text-slate-800">
                  Pilih warga
                </div>

                <p className="mx-auto mt-1 max-w-sm text-sm leading-6 text-slate-500">
                  Pilih salah satu warga dari hasil pencarian untuk membuka form
                  pemeriksaan.
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}

FILE: src/components/kk/create/create-kk-dialog.tsx
############################################################

import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod'
import { IdCard, Loader2, Save, UsersRound } from 'lucide-react'
import { useEffect } from 'react'
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form'
import { toast } from 'sonner'

import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import {
  Dialog,
  DialogContent,
  DialogDescription,
  DialogFooter,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogTitle,
} from '@/components/ui/dialog'

import { kkSchema } from '@/lib/kk-parser/schema'
import type { ValidatedKK } from '@/lib/kk-parser/schema'

import { saveKK } from '@/server/kk.functions'

import { createEmptyKK, normalizeKKFormValues } from '../form/kk-form-defaults'

import { KKFormFields } from '../form/kk-form-fields'

type Props = {
  open: boolean

  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void

  onCreated?: () => void | Promise<void>

  initialValues?: ValidatedKK | null
}

export function CreateKKDialog({
  open,
  onOpenChange,
  onCreated,
  initialValues,
}: Props) {
  const form = useForm<ValidatedKK>({
    resolver: zodResolver(kkSchema),

    defaultValues: createEmptyKK(),
  })

  const {
    reset,
    handleSubmit,

    formState: { isSubmitting },
  } = form

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) {
      return
    }

    reset(initialValues ?? createEmptyKK())
  }, [open, initialValues, reset])

  function resetForm() {
    reset(createEmptyKK())
  }

  function handleOpenChange(nextOpen: boolean) {
    if (!nextOpen && isSubmitting) {
      return
    }

    onOpenChange(nextOpen)
  }

  async function onSubmit(values: ValidatedKK) {
    const normalizedValues = normalizeKKFormValues(values)

    try {
      const response = await saveKK({
        data: normalizedValues,
      })

      toast.success('Data berhasil disimpan', {
        description: `${response.memberCount.toLocaleString(
          'id-ID',
        )} anggota keluarga berhasil disimpan.`,
      })

      resetForm()

      onOpenChange(false)

      await onCreated?.()
    } catch (error) {
      const message =
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : 'Terjadi kesalahan pada server.'

      toast.error('Gagal menyimpan data', {
        description: message,
      })
    }
  }

  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={handleOpenChange}>
      <DialogContent
        className="
          flex
          max-h-[92dvh]
          w-[calc(100vw-1.5rem)]
          max-w-[calc(100vw-1.5rem)]
          flex-col
          gap-0
          overflow-hidden
          rounded-2xl
          border-slate-200
          bg-white
          p-0
          shadow-xl
          sm:w-[calc(100vw-3rem)]
          sm:max-w-5xl
        "
      >
        {/* HEADER */}
        <DialogHeader className="shrink-0 border-b border-slate-200 bg-white px-5 py-4 pr-12 sm:px-6 sm:py-5">
          <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
            <div className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
              <IdCard className="size-4" />
            </div>

            <div className="min-w-0">
              <DialogTitle className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 sm:text-lg">
                Tambah Kartu Keluarga
              </DialogTitle>

              <DialogDescription className="mt-1 max-w-2xl text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500 sm:text-sm">
                Masukkan data Kartu Keluarga beserta anggota keluarga yang
                terdaftar di dalamnya.
              </DialogDescription>
            </div>
          </div>
        </DialogHeader>

        {/* FORM AREA */}
        <form
          id="create-kk-form"
          onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)}
          className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-x-hidden overflow-y-auto bg-slate-50/40"
        >
          <div className="p-4 sm:p-6">
            <KKFormFields form={form} disabled={isSubmitting} />
          </div>
        </form>

        {/* FOOTER */}
        <DialogFooter className="shrink-0 border-t border-slate-200 bg-white px-4 pt-4 pb-5 sm:px-6 sm:pt-4 sm:pb-6">
          <div className="flex w-full flex-col-reverse gap-2 sm:flex-row sm:justify-end">
            <Button
              type="button"
              variant="outline"
              className="h-10 rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white px-5 shadow-none"
              disabled={isSubmitting}
              onClick={() => handleOpenChange(false)}
            >
              Batal
            </Button>

            <Button
              type="submit"
              form="create-kk-form"
              className="h-10 rounded-lg px-5 sm:min-w-40"
              disabled={isSubmitting}
            >
              {isSubmitting ? (
                <>
                  <Loader2 className="size-4 animate-spin" />
                  Menyimpan...
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <Save className="size-4" />
                  Simpan Data
                </>
              )}
            </Button>
          </div>
        </DialogFooter>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  )
}

FILE: src/components/kk/detail/gender-badge.tsx
############################################################

import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'

type Props = {
  value: string | null
}

export function GenderBadge({ value }: Props) {
  if (!value) {
    return <span className="text-sm text-slate-400">-</span>
  }

  const normalized = value.trim().toLowerCase()

  const isMale = normalized === 'laki-laki' || normalized === 'l'

  const isFemale = normalized === 'perempuan' || normalized === 'p'

  return (
    <Badge
      variant="outline"
      className={[
        'whitespace-nowrap rounded-full px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-medium shadow-none',
        isMale
          ? 'border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 text-blue-700'
          : isFemale
            ? 'border-rose-200 bg-rose-50 text-rose-700'
            : 'border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 text-slate-600',
      ].join(' ')}
    >
      {value}
    </Badge>
  )
}
