############################################################
FILE: src/hooks/use-kk-parser.ts
############################################################

import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'

import type { KKData } from '@/lib/kk-parser/types'
import { createMaskedKK } from '@/lib/kk-parser/mask'
import { parseKK } from '@/lib/kk-parser/parse-kk'
import { kkSchema } from '@/lib/kk-parser/schema'

import { saveKK } from '@/server/kk.functions'
import { toast } from 'sonner'

type ValidationIssue = {
  path: readonly PropertyKey[]
  message: string
}

function formatValidationErrors(issues: readonly ValidationIssue[]): string[] {
  return issues.map((issue) => {
    const field =
      issue.path.length > 0 ? issue.path.map(String).join('.') : 'data'

    return `${field}: ${issue.message}`
  })
}

export function useKKParser() {
  const [text, setTextState] = useState('')

  const [result, setResult] = useState<KKData | null>(null)

  const [parseError, setParseError] = useState<string | null>(null)

  const [validationErrors, setValidationErrors] = useState<string[]>([])

  const [isValid, setIsValid] = useState(false)

  const [showSensitive, setShowSensitive] = useState(false)

  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false)

  const [isSaved, setIsSaved] = useState(false)

  const previewData = useMemo(() => {
    if (!result) {
      return null
    }

    return showSensitive ? result : createMaskedKK(result)
  }, [result, showSensitive])

  function resetResultState() {
    setResult(null)
    setParseError(null)
    setValidationErrors([])
    setIsValid(false)
    setShowSensitive(false)
    setIsSaved(false)
  }

  function setText(value: string) {
    setTextState(value)

    // Kalau raw text berubah setelah diparse,
    // hasil lama tidak lagi dianggap valid.
    if (result) {
      resetResultState()
    }
  }

  function handleParse() {
    setParseError(null)
    setResult(null)
    setValidationErrors([])
    setIsValid(false)
    setShowSensitive(false)
    setIsSaved(false)

    if (!text.trim()) {
      setParseError('Text Kartu Keluarga belum diisi.')
      return
    }

    try {
      const parsed = parseKK(text)

      setResult(parsed)

      const validation = kkSchema.safeParse(parsed)

      if (!validation.success) {
        setValidationErrors(formatValidationErrors(validation.error.issues))

        return
      }

      setIsValid(true)
    } catch {
      setParseError('Terjadi kesalahan saat melakukan parsing text.')
    }
  }

  function handleClear() {
    setTextState('')
    resetResultState()
    setIsSaving(false)
  }

  async function handleCopy() {
    if (!previewData) {
      return
    }

    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(JSON.stringify(previewData, null, 2))

      toast.success('JSON berhasil disalin')
    } catch {
      toast.error('Gagal menyalin JSON')
    }
  }

  async function handleSave() {
    if (!result || isSaving) {
      return
    }

    setIsSaving(true)

    try {
      const validation = kkSchema.safeParse(result)

      if (!validation.success) {
        const errors = formatValidationErrors(validation.error.issues)

        setValidationErrors(errors)
        setIsValid(false)

        toast.error('Data belum dapat disimpan', {
          description:
            'Data tidak lolos validasi. Periksa kembali hasil parsing.',
        })

        return
      }

      const response = await saveKK({
        data: validation.data,
      })

      setIsSaved(true)

      toast.success('Data berhasil disimpan', {
        description: `${response.memberCount} anggota keluarga berhasil disimpan ke database.`,
      })
    } catch (err) {
      const message =
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Terjadi kesalahan pada server.'

      toast.error('Gagal menyimpan data', {
        description: message,
      })
    } finally {
      setIsSaving(false)
    }
  }

  function toggleSensitive() {
    if (!result) {
      return
    }

    setShowSensitive((current) => !current)
  }

  return {
    text,
    result,
    previewData,

    parseError,
    validationErrors,

    isValid,
    showSensitive,

    isSaving,
    isSaved,

    canClear: Boolean(text || result || parseError),

    canSave: Boolean(result) && isValid && !isSaving && !isSaved,

    setText,
    handleParse,
    handleClear,
    handleCopy,
    handleSave,
    toggleSensitive,
  }
}


############################################################
FILE: src/hooks/use-list-controller.ts
############################################################

/* eslint-disable @typescript-eslint/no-unnecessary-type-assertion */
import { useCallback, useMemo } from 'react'

import type { SortingState } from '@tanstack/react-table'

import type { PageSize } from '@/components/data-list/data-pagination'

type SearchState = {
  page?: number
  limit?: PageSize

  q?: string
  by?: string

  sort?: string
  order?: 'asc' | 'desc'
}

type Props<TSearch extends SearchState> = {
  search: TSearch

  defaultSort: string
  defaultOrder?: 'asc' | 'desc'

  allowedSorts: ReadonlySet<string>

  navigate: (options: {
    search: (previous: TSearch) => TSearch
  }) => void | Promise<void>
}

export function useListController<TSearch extends SearchState>({
  search,
  defaultSort,
  defaultOrder = 'asc',
  allowedSorts,
  navigate,
}: Props<TSearch>) {
  const sorting: SortingState = useMemo(
    () => [
      {
        id: search.sort ?? defaultSort,

        desc: (search.order ?? defaultOrder) === 'desc',
      },
    ],
    [search.sort, search.order, defaultSort, defaultOrder],
  )

  const searchChange = useCallback(
    (value: string) => {
      void navigate({
        search: (previous) =>
          ({
            ...previous,

            page: undefined,

            q: value.trim() || undefined,
          }) as TSearch,
      })
    },
    [navigate],
  )

  const searchByChange = useCallback(
    (value: string) => {
      void navigate({
        search: (previous) =>
          ({
            ...previous,

            page: undefined,

            by: value === 'all' ? undefined : value,
          }) as TSearch,
      })
    },
    [navigate],
  )

  const sortingChange = useCallback(
    (updater: SortingState | ((old: SortingState) => SortingState)) => {
      const next = typeof updater === 'function' ? updater(sorting) : updater

      const first = next[0]

      if (!first || !allowedSorts.has(first.id)) {
        return
      }

      void navigate({
        search: (previous) =>
          ({
            ...previous,

            page: undefined,

            sort: first.id,

            order: first.desc ? 'desc' : 'asc',
          }) as TSearch,
      })
    },
    [allowedSorts, navigate, sorting],
  )

  const pageChange = useCallback(
    (page: number) => {
      void navigate({
        search: (previous) =>
          ({
            ...previous,

            page: page <= 1 ? undefined : page,
          }) as TSearch,
      })
    },
    [navigate],
  )

  const pageSizeChange = useCallback(
    (limit: PageSize) => {
      void navigate({
        search: (previous) =>
          ({
            ...previous,

            page: undefined,

            limit: limit === 20 ? undefined : limit,
          }) as TSearch,
      })
    },
    [navigate],
  )

  return {
    sorting,

    searchChange,
    searchByChange,
    sortingChange,

    pageChange,
    pageSizeChange,
  }
}


############################################################
FILE: src/hooks/use-sensitive-kk.ts
############################################################

import { useState } from 'react'

import { getKKPrivateLocal } from '@/server/kk.local.functions'

type PrivateKK = Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getKKPrivateLocal>>

export function useSensitiveKK(id: number) {
  const [data, setData] = useState<PrivateKK | null>(null)

  const [isVisible, setIsVisible] = useState(false)

  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false)

  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)

  async function toggle() {
    if (isVisible) {
      hide()
      return
    }

    setIsLoading(true)
    setError(null)

    try {
      const response = await getKKPrivateLocal({
        data: {
          id,
        },
      })

      setData(response)
      setIsVisible(true)
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Gagal membuka data sensitif.',
      )
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false)
    }
  }

  function hide() {
    setIsVisible(false)

    // Plaintext langsung dibuang dari React state.
    setData(null)
  }

  function getNik(memberNo: number, maskedNik: string) {
    if (!isVisible || !data) {
      return maskedNik
    }

    return (
      data.kk.anggota.find((member) => member.no === memberNo)?.nik ?? maskedNik
    )
  }

  function getNoKK(maskedNoKK: string) {
    if (!isVisible || !data) {
      return maskedNoKK
    }

    return data.kk.noKK
  }

  return {
    data,
    isVisible,
    isLoading,
    error,

    toggle,
    hide,
    getNik,
    getNoKK,
  }
}


############################################################
FILE: src/lib/kk-parser/mask.ts
############################################################

import type { KKData } from './types'

function maskNumber(value: string | null) {
  if (!value) {
    return value
  }

  if (value.length <= 4) {
    return '*'.repeat(value.length)
  }

  return '*'.repeat(value.length - 4) + value.slice(-4)
}

export function createMaskedKK(data: KKData): KKData {
  return {
    ...data,

    noKK: maskNumber(data.noKK),

    anggota: data.anggota.map((member) => ({
      ...member,
      nik: maskNumber(member.nik) ?? '',
    })),
  }
}


############################################################
FILE: src/lib/kk-parser/normalize.ts
############################################################

export function normalizeText(raw: string): string[] {
  return raw
    .replace(/\r/g, '')
    .split('\n')
    .map((line) => line.trim())
    .filter((line) => line.length > 0)
}

export function cleanValue(value?: string | null): string | null {
  if (!value) {
    return null
  }

  return value.replace(/^:\s*/, '').trim()
}

export function cleanSpaces(value: string): string {
  return value.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim()
}


############################################################
FILE: src/lib/kk-parser/parse-header.ts
############################################################

import type { KKData } from './types'

type HeaderData = Omit<KKData, 'anggota'>

export function parseHeader(rawText: string): HeaderData {
  const lines = normalizeLines(rawText)

  /*
   * Hanya ambil bagian header.
   * Berhenti ketika menemukan
   * anggota pertama.
   */
  const headerLines = getHeaderLines(lines)

  const noKK = findNoKK(headerLines)

  const kepalaKeluarga = findFieldValue(headerLines, [
    'NAMA KEPALA KELUARGA',

    /*
     * OCR sering salah:
     * KEPALA → KEPAIA
     */
    'NAMA KEPAIA KELUARGA',
  ])

  const alamat = findFieldValue(headerLines, ['ALAMAT'])

  const rtRw = findFieldValue(headerLines, ['RT/RW', 'RT / RW'])

  const { rt, rw } = parseRtRw(rtRw)

  const desaKelurahan = findFieldValue(headerLines, [
    'DESA/KELURAHAN',
    'DESA / KELURAHAN',
  ])

  const kecamatan = findFieldValue(headerLines, ['KECAMATAN'])

  const kodePos = findFieldValue(headerLines, ['KODE POS'])

  const kabupatenKota = findFieldValue(headerLines, [
    'KABUPATEN/KOTA',
    'KABUPATEN / KOTA',
  ])

  const provinsi = findFieldValue(headerLines, ['PROVINSI'])

  return {
    noKK,

    kepalaKeluarga,

    alamat: nullable(alamat),

    rt,

    rw,

    desaKelurahan: nullable(desaKelurahan),

    kecamatan: nullable(kecamatan),

    kabupatenKota: nullable(kabupatenKota),

    kodePos: nullable(cleanDigits(kodePos ?? '')),

    provinsi: nullable(provinsi),
  }
}

/* ======================================================
 * HEADER BOUNDARY
 * ====================================================== */

function getHeaderLines(lines: string[]) {
  /*
   * Cari:
   *
   * 1
   * TES1
   * 331220...
   *
   * Jangan menganggap nomor lain
   * sebagai awal anggota.
   */
  for (let index = 0; index < lines.length; index += 1) {
    if (!isRowNumber(lines[index])) {
      continue
    }

    const nearby = lines.slice(index + 1, index + 6)

    const hasNik = nearby.some(isIdentityNumber)

    if (hasNik) {
      return lines.slice(0, index)
    }
  }

  return lines
}

/* ======================================================
 * NO KK
 * ====================================================== */

function findNoKK(lines: string[]): string | null {
  for (let index = 0; index < lines.length; index += 1) {
    const line = lines[index]

    /*
     * No. 3312202506090002
     */
    const inline = line.match(/^NO\s*\.?\s*:?\s*(\d{10,20})$/i)

    if (inline?.[1]) {
      return inline[1]
    }

    /*
     * No.
     * 3312202506090002
     */
    if (/^NO\s*\.?\s*:?\s*$/i.test(line)) {
      const next = cleanDigits(lines[index + 1] ?? '')

      if (isIdentityNumber(next)) {
        return next
      }
    }
  }

  return null
}

/* ======================================================
 * GENERIC HEADER FIELD
 * ====================================================== */

function findFieldValue(lines: string[], labels: string[]): string | null {
  for (let index = 0; index < lines.length; index += 1) {
    const current = normalizeSemantic(lines[index])

    for (const label of labels) {
      const normalizedLabel = normalizeSemantic(label)

      /*
       * Format:
       *
       * Alamat : JOHO
       */
      if (current.startsWith(`${normalizedLabel}:`)) {
        const separator = lines[index].indexOf(':')

        if (separator >= 0) {
          const value = cleanValue(lines[index].slice(separator + 1))

          if (value) {
            return value
          }
        }
      }

      /*
       * Format:
       *
       * Alamat
       * : JOHO
       */
      if (current === normalizedLabel) {
        for (
          let nextIndex = index + 1;
          nextIndex < Math.min(lines.length, index + 4);
          nextIndex += 1
        ) {
          const next = cleanValue(lines[nextIndex])

          if (!next) {
            continue
          }

          /*
           * Kalau baris selanjutnya
           * ternyata label baru,
           * jangan dipakai sebagai value.
           */
          if (isKnownHeaderLabel(next)) {
            break
          }

          return next
        }
      }
    }
  }

  return null
}

/* ======================================================
 * RT / RW
 * ====================================================== */

function parseRtRw(value: string | null) {
  if (!value) {
    return {
      rt: null,
      rw: null,
    }
  }

  const match = value.match(/(\d{1,3})\s*\/\s*(\d{1,3})/)

  if (!match) {
    return {
      rt: null,
      rw: null,
    }
  }

  return {
    rt: match[1],
    rw: match[2],
  }
}

/* ======================================================
 * HEADER LABEL
 * ====================================================== */

function isKnownHeaderLabel(value: string) {
  const normalized = normalizeSemantic(value)

  const labels = [
    'KARTU KELUARGA',

    'NAMA KEPALA KELUARGA',
    'NAMA KEPAIA KELUARGA',

    'ALAMAT',

    'RT/RW',
    'RT / RW',

    'DESA/KELURAHAN',
    'DESA / KELURAHAN',

    'KECAMATAN',

    'KODE POS',

    'KABUPATEN/KOTA',
    'KABUPATEN / KOTA',

    'PROVINSI',
  ]

  return labels.some((label) => normalized === normalizeSemantic(label))
}

/* ======================================================
 * UTILITY
 * ====================================================== */

function normalizeLines(value: string) {
  return value.replace(/\r/g, '').split('\n').map(cleanValue).filter(Boolean)
}

function cleanValue(value: string) {
  return value
    .replace(/^[\s:;|]+/, '')
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
    .trim()
}

function normalizeSemantic(value: string) {
  return cleanValue(value)
    .toUpperCase()
    .replace(/–|—/g, '-')
    .replace(/\s*\/\s*/g, '/')
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
    .trim()
}

function cleanDigits(value: string) {
  return value.replace(/\D/g, '')
}

function isIdentityNumber(value: string) {
  const digits = cleanDigits(value)

  return digits === value.trim() && digits.length >= 10 && digits.length <= 20
}

function isRowNumber(value: string) {
  return /^\d{1,2}$/.test(value.trim())
}

function nullable(value: string | null) {
  if (!value) {
    return null
  }

  const result = value.trim()

  return result ? result : null
}


############################################################
FILE: src/lib/kk-parser/parse-kk.ts
############################################################

import type { KKData } from './types'

import { parseHeader } from './parse-header'

import { parseMembers } from './parse-members'

export function parseKK(rawText: string): KKData {
  const header = parseHeader(rawText)

  const anggota = parseMembers(rawText)

  return {
    ...header,
    anggota,
  }
}


############################################################
FILE: src/lib/kk-parser/parse-members.ts
############################################################

import type { KKMember } from './types'

const RELIGIONS = new Set([
  'ISLAM',
  'KRISTEN',
  'KRISTEN PROTESTAN',
  'PROTESTAN',
  'KATOLIK',
  'KATHOLIK',
  'HINDU',
  'BUDDHA',
  'BUDHA',
  'KONGHUCU',
  'KEPERCAYAAN',
])

const BLOOD_TYPES = new Set([
  'A',
  'B',
  'AB',
  'O',
  'A+',
  'A-',
  'B+',
  'B-',
  'AB+',
  'AB-',
  'O+',
  'O-',
  'TIDAK TAHU',
  'TIDAK DIKETAHUI',
])

const EDUCATION_PATTERNS = [
  'TIDAK/BELUM SEKOLAH',
  'BELUM TAMAT SD/SEDERAJAT',
  'TAMAT SD/SEDERAJAT',
  'SD/SEDERAJAT',

  'SLTP/SEDERAJAT',
  'SMP/SEDERAJAT',

  'SLTA/SEDERAJAT',
  'SMA/SEDERAJAT',
  'SMK/SEDERAJAT',

  'DIPLOMA I',
  'DIPLOMA II',
  'DIPLOMA III',
  'DIPLOMA IV',

  'AKADEMI',

  'STRATA I',
  'STRATA II',
  'STRATA III',

  'S1',
  'S2',
  'S3',
]

const OCCUPATIONS = new Set([
  'BELUM/TIDAK BEKERJA',
  'MENGURUS RUMAH TANGGA',

  'PELAJAR/MAHASISWA',
  'PELAJAR',
  'MAHASISWA',

  'PENSIUNAN',

  'PEGAWAI NEGERI SIPIL',
  'PNS',

  'TNI',
  'POLRI',

  'PETANI',
  'PETANI/PEKEBUN',
  'PEKEBUN',

  'PETERNAK',

  'NELAYAN',
  'NELAYAN/PERIKANAN',

  'KARYAWAN SWASTA',
  'KARYAWAN BUMN',
  'KARYAWAN BUMD',
  'KARYAWAN HONORER',

  'BURUH HARIAN LEPAS',

  'PEDAGANG',

  'GURU',
  'DOSEN',

  'DOKTER',
  'PERAWAT',
  'BIDAN',

  'SOPIR',

  'PERANGKAT DESA',

  'KEPALA DESA',

  'WIRASWASTA',

  'LAINNYA',
])

export function parseMembers(rawText: string): KKMember[] {
  const lines = normalizeLines(rawText)

  const numbered = parseNumberedMembers(lines)
  const nikAnchored = parseNikAnchoredMembers(lines)

  if (numbered.length === 0) {
    return nikAnchored
  }

  if (nikAnchored.length > numbered.length) {
    return nikAnchored
  }

  return numbered
}

/* ======================================================
 * NUMBERED MEMBER MODE
 * ====================================================== */

function parseNumberedMembers(lines: string[]): KKMember[] {
  const starts: number[] = []

  for (let index = 0; index < lines.length; index += 1) {
    if (!isRowNumber(lines[index])) {
      continue
    }

    /*
     * Dalam beberapa baris berikutnya
     * harus ada NIK.
     */
    const nearby = lines.slice(index + 1, index + 7)

    if (nearby.some(isIdentityNumber)) {
      starts.push(index)
    }
  }

  if (starts.length === 0) {
    return []
  }

  const members: KKMember[] = []

  for (let index = 0; index < starts.length; index += 1) {
    const start = starts[index]

    const end = starts[index + 1] ?? lines.length

    const block = lines.slice(start + 1, end)

    const member = parseMemberBlock(block, members.length + 1)

    if (member) {
      members.push(member)
    }
  }

  return members
}

function parseMemberBlock(block: string[], no: number): KKMember | null {
  if (block.length === 0) {
    return null
  }

  const nikIndex = block.findIndex(isIdentityNumber)

  if (nikIndex < 0) {
    return null
  }

  const nik = cleanDigits(block[nikIndex])

  const genderIndex = block.findIndex(
    (value) => normalizeGender(value) !== null,
  )

  const gender = genderIndex >= 0 ? normalizeGender(block[genderIndex]) : null

  const dateIndex = block.findIndex((value) => normalizeDate(value) !== null)

  const tanggalLahir = dateIndex >= 0 ? normalizeDate(block[dateIndex]) : null

  const religionIndex = block.findIndex(
    (value) => normalizeReligion(value) !== null,
  )

  const agama =
    religionIndex >= 0 ? normalizeReligion(block[religionIndex]) : null

  const educationIndex = block.findIndex(
    (value) => normalizeEducation(value) !== null,
  )

  const pendidikan =
    educationIndex >= 0 ? normalizeEducation(block[educationIndex]) : null

  const occupationIndex = block.findIndex(
    (value) => normalizeOccupation(value) !== null,
  )

  const pekerjaan =
    occupationIndex >= 0 ? normalizeOccupation(block[occupationIndex]) : null

  const bloodIndex = block.findIndex(
    (value) => normalizeBloodType(value) !== null,
  )

  const golonganDarah =
    bloodIndex >= 0 ? normalizeBloodType(block[bloodIndex]) : null

  /*
   * Nama tidak boleh bergantung
   * apakah NIK berada sebelum/atau
   * sesudah jenis kelamin.
   *
   * Contoh anggota 3:
   *
   * TES3
   * PEREMPUAN
   * 331220...
   */
  const namaIndex = findNameIndex(block, {
    nikIndex,
    genderIndex,
    dateIndex,
    religionIndex,
    educationIndex,
    occupationIndex,
    bloodIndex,
  })

  const nama = namaIndex >= 0 ? cleanText(block[namaIndex]) : ''

  /*
   * Tempat lahir adalah field text
   * bebas yang tersisa setelah
   * field semantic dibuang.
   */
  const tempatLahir = findBirthPlace(
    block,
    new Set([
      nikIndex,
      genderIndex,
      dateIndex,
      religionIndex,
      educationIndex,
      occupationIndex,
      bloodIndex,
      namaIndex,
    ]),
  )

  return {
    no,

    nama,

    nik,

    jenisKelamin: gender,

    tempatLahir,

    tanggalLahir,

    agama,

    pendidikan,

    pekerjaan,

    golonganDarah,
  }
}

/* ======================================================
 * NAME
 * ====================================================== */

function findNameIndex(
  block: string[],
  used: {
    nikIndex: number
    genderIndex: number
    dateIndex: number
    religionIndex: number
    educationIndex: number
    occupationIndex: number
    bloodIndex: number
  },
) {
  const excluded = new Set([
    used.nikIndex,
    used.genderIndex,
    used.dateIndex,
    used.religionIndex,
    used.educationIndex,
    used.occupationIndex,
    used.bloodIndex,
  ])

  /*
   * Nama biasanya field text
   * pertama setelah nomor anggota.
   */
  for (let index = 0; index < block.length; index += 1) {
    if (excluded.has(index)) {
      continue
    }

    const value = cleanText(block[index])

    if (!isFreeText(value)) {
      continue
    }

    return index
  }

  return -1
}

/* ======================================================
 * BIRTH PLACE
 * ====================================================== */

function findBirthPlace(block: string[], excluded: Set<number>): string | null {
  for (let index = 0; index < block.length; index += 1) {
    if (excluded.has(index)) {
      continue
    }

    const value = cleanText(block[index])

    if (!isFreeText(value)) {
      continue
    }

    /*
     * Remaining free text pertama
     * setelah nama dianggap
     * tempat lahir.
     */
    return value
  }

  return null
}

/* ======================================================
 * FALLBACK: NIK ANCHOR MODE
 * ====================================================== */

function parseNikAnchoredMembers(lines: string[]): KKMember[] {
  const nikIndexes: number[] = []

  for (let index = 0; index < lines.length; index += 1) {
    if (isIdentityNumber(lines[index])) {
      /*
       * Jangan masukkan No KK.
       *
       * No KK biasanya berada
       * sebelum area anggota.
       */
      if (looksLikeNoKKContext(lines, index)) {
        continue
      }

      nikIndexes.push(index)
    }
  }

  if (nikIndexes.length === 0) {
    return []
  }

  return nikIndexes.map((nikIndex, memberIndex) => {
    const previousNik = nikIndexes[memberIndex - 1] ?? -1

    const nextNik = nikIndexes[memberIndex + 1] ?? lines.length

    const start = Math.max(previousNik + 1, nikIndex - 4)

    const end = Math.min(nextNik, nikIndex + 12)

    const block = lines.slice(start, end)

    /*
     * Tambahkan nomor dummy
     * lalu gunakan parser block.
     */
    const parsed = parseMemberBlock(block, memberIndex + 1)

    if (parsed) {
      return parsed
    }

    return {
      no: memberIndex + 1,

      nama: '',

      nik: cleanDigits(lines[nikIndex]),

      jenisKelamin: null,

      tempatLahir: null,

      tanggalLahir: null,

      agama: null,

      pendidikan: null,

      pekerjaan: null,

      golonganDarah: null,
    }
  })
}

/* ======================================================
 * GENDER
 * ====================================================== */

function normalizeGender(value: string): 'LAKI-LAKI' | 'PEREMPUAN' | null {
  const normalized = normalizeSemantic(value)

  if (normalized === 'PEREMPUAN') {
    return 'PEREMPUAN'
  }

  if (normalized === 'LAKI-LAKI' || normalized === 'LAKI LAKI') {
    return 'LAKI-LAKI'
  }

  return null
}

/* ======================================================
 * DATE
 * ====================================================== */

function normalizeDate(value: string): string | null {
  const normalized = value.trim().replace(/[./]/g, '-').replace(/\s+/g, '')

  const match = normalized.match(/^(\d{1,2})-(\d{1,2})-(\d{4})$/)

  if (!match) {
    return null
  }

  const day = Number(match[1])

  const month = Number(match[2])

  const year = Number(match[3])

  if (
    day < 1 ||
    day > 31 ||
    month < 1 ||
    month > 12 ||
    year < 1900 ||
    year > 2100
  ) {
    return null
  }

  return [
    String(day).padStart(2, '0'),

    String(month).padStart(2, '0'),

    String(year),
  ].join('-')
}

/* ======================================================
 * RELIGION
 * ====================================================== */

function normalizeReligion(value: string): string | null {
  const normalized = normalizeSemantic(value)

  if (!RELIGIONS.has(normalized)) {
    return null
  }

  if (normalized === 'KATHOLIK') {
    return 'KATOLIK'
  }

  if (normalized === 'BUDHA') {
    return 'BUDDHA'
  }

  return normalized
}

/* ======================================================
 * EDUCATION
 * ====================================================== */

function normalizeEducation(value: string): string | null {
  const normalized = normalizeSemantic(value)

  for (const education of EDUCATION_PATTERNS) {
    if (normalized === education) {
      return normalized
    }
  }

  return null
}

/* ======================================================
 * OCCUPATION
 * ====================================================== */

function normalizeOccupation(value: string): string | null {
  const normalized = normalizeSemantic(value)

  if (OCCUPATIONS.has(normalized)) {
    return normalized
  }

  return null
}

/* ======================================================
 * BLOOD TYPE
 * ====================================================== */

function normalizeBloodType(value: string): string | null {
  const normalized = normalizeSemantic(value)

  if (BLOOD_TYPES.has(normalized)) {
    return normalized
  }

  return null
}

/* ======================================================
 * FREE TEXT
 * ====================================================== */

function isFreeText(value: string) {
  if (!value) {
    return false
  }

  if (
    isIdentityNumber(value) ||
    isRowNumber(value) ||
    normalizeGender(value) ||
    normalizeDate(value) ||
    normalizeReligion(value) ||
    normalizeEducation(value) ||
    normalizeOccupation(value) ||
    normalizeBloodType(value)
  ) {
    return false
  }

  if (isKnownHeaderOrTableLabel(value)) {
    return false
  }

  return /[A-Za-z]/.test(value)
}

/* ======================================================
 * LABELS / JUNK
 * ====================================================== */

function isKnownHeaderOrTableLabel(value: string) {
  const normalized = normalizeSemantic(value)

  const labels = [
    'KARTU KELUARGA',
    'REPUBLIK INDONESIA',

    'NAMA KEPALA KELUARGA',
    'NAMA KEPAIA KELUARGA',

    'ALAMAT',
    'RT/RW',

    'DESA/KELURAHAN',
    'KECAMATAN',
    'KODE POS',

    'KABUPATEN/KOTA',
    'PROVINSI',

    'NO',
    'NAMA LENGKAP',
    'NIK',

    'JENIS KELAMIN',
    'TEMPAT LAHIR',
    'TANGGAL LAHIR',

    'AGAMA',
    'PENDIDIKAN',

    'JENIS PEKERJAAN',

    'GOLONGAN DARAH',
  ]

  return labels.includes(normalized)
}

/* ======================================================
 * NIK
 * ====================================================== */

function isIdentityNumber(value: string) {
  const trimmed = value.trim()

  /*
   * Development:
   * dummy NIK masih boleh
   * 10 sampai 20 digit.
   */
  return /^\d{10,20}$/.test(trimmed)
}

function looksLikeNoKKContext(lines: string[], index: number) {
  const previous = normalizeSemantic(lines[index - 1] ?? '')

  const twoBefore = normalizeSemantic(lines[index - 2] ?? '')

  return (
    previous === 'NO' ||
    previous === 'KARTU KELUARGA' ||
    twoBefore === 'KARTU KELUARGA'
  )
}

function cleanDigits(value: string) {
  return value.replace(/\D/g, '')
}

/* ======================================================
 * GENERAL
 * ====================================================== */

function normalizeLines(value: string) {
  return value.replace(/\r/g, '').split('\n').map(cleanText).filter(Boolean)
}

function cleanText(value: string) {
  return value
    .replace(/^[\s:;|]+/, '')
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
    .trim()
}

function normalizeSemantic(value: string) {
  return cleanText(value)
    .toUpperCase()
    .replace(/–|—/g, '-')
    .replace(/\s*\/\s*/g, '/')
    .replace(/\s*-\s*/g, '-')
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
    .trim()
}

function isRowNumber(value: string) {
  return /^[1-9]\d?$/.test(value.trim())
}

import { z } from 'zod'

const nullableText = z.string().trim().nullable()

export const kkMemberSchema = z.object({
  no: z.number().int().positive(),

  nama: z.string().trim().min(1, 'Nama tidak boleh kosong'),

  nik: z
    .string()
    .trim()
    .regex(/^\d{16}$/, 'NIK harus 16 digit'),

  jenisKelamin: z.enum(['LAKI-LAKI', 'PEREMPUAN']).nullable(),

  tempatLahir: nullableText,

  tanggalLahir: z
    .string()
    .regex(/^\d{2}-\d{2}-\d{4}$/, 'Tanggal lahir harus DD-MM-YYYY')
    .nullable(),

  agama: nullableText,
  pendidikan: nullableText,
  pekerjaan: nullableText,
  golonganDarah: nullableText,
})

export const kkSchema = z.object({
  noKK: z
    .string()
    .trim()
    .regex(/^\d{16}$/, 'Nomor KK harus 16 digit'),

  kepalaKeluarga: z
    .string()
    .trim()
    .min(1, 'Kepala keluarga tidak boleh kosong'),

  alamat: nullableText,

  rt: z
    .string()
    .regex(/^\d{1,3}$/)
    .nullable(),

  rw: z
    .string()
    .regex(/^\d{1,3}$/)
    .nullable(),

  desaKelurahan: nullableText,
  kecamatan: nullableText,
  kabupatenKota: nullableText,

  kodePos: z
    .string()
    .regex(/^\d{5}$/, 'Kode pos harus 5 digit')
    .nullable(),

  provinsi: nullableText,

  anggota: z.array(kkMemberSchema).min(1, 'Tidak ada anggota keluarga'),
})

export type ValidatedKK = z.infer<typeof kkSchema>

############################################################
FILE: src/lib/list-search.ts
############################################################

export type PageSize = 10 | 20 | 50 | 100

export type SortOrder = 'asc' | 'desc'

export type ListSearch<TSearchBy extends string, TSortBy extends string> = {
  page?: number
  limit?: PageSize

  q?: string
  by?: TSearchBy

  sort?: TSortBy
  order?: SortOrder
}

type Options<TSearchBy extends string, TSortBy extends string> = {
  searchBy: readonly TSearchBy[]
  sortBy: readonly TSortBy[]
}

const allowedPageSizes = new Set<number>([10, 20, 50, 100])

export function createListSearchValidator<
  TSearchBy extends string,
  TSortBy extends string,
>({ searchBy, sortBy }: Options<TSearchBy, TSortBy>) {
  const allowedSearchBy = new Set<string>(searchBy)

  const allowedSortBy = new Set<string>(sortBy)

  return (search: Record<string, unknown>): ListSearch<TSearchBy, TSortBy> => {
    const result: ListSearch<TSearchBy, TSortBy> = {}

    const page = Number(search.page)

    if (Number.isInteger(page) && page > 1) {
      result.page = page
    }

    const limit = Number(search.limit)

    if (allowedPageSizes.has(limit)) {
      result.limit = limit as PageSize
    }

    if (typeof search.q === 'string') {
      const q = search.q.trim()

      if (q) {
        result.q = q
      }
    }

    if (typeof search.by === 'string' && allowedSearchBy.has(search.by)) {
      result.by = search.by as TSearchBy
    }

    if (typeof search.sort === 'string' && allowedSortBy.has(search.sort)) {
      result.sort = search.sort as TSortBy
    }

    if (search.order === 'asc' || search.order === 'desc') {
      result.order = search.order
    }

    return result
  }
}

        </div>

        <section className="mb-6 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
          <div className="h-1 bg-linear-to-r from-blue-600 via-blue-500 to-sky-400" />

          <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 p-5 sm:p-6 lg:flex-row lg:items-start lg:justify-between">
            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
              <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
                <div className="mt-0.5 flex size-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600 ring-1 ring-inset ring-blue-100">
                  <UserRound className="size-5" />
                </div>

                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <div className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-blue-600">
                    Data Anggota
                  </div>

                  <h1 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900 sm:text-[28px]">
                    {anggota.nama}
                  </h1>

                  <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-x-5 gap-y-2 text-sm text-slate-500">
                    <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                      <IdCard className="size-4 text-slate-400" />

                      <span className="font-mono tabular-nums">
                        NIK {anggota.nik}
                      </span>
                    </span>

                    <span className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1.5">
                      <MapPin className="size-4 shrink-0 text-slate-400" />

                      <span className="truncate">
                        {lokasi || 'Lokasi belum tersedia'}
                      </span>
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="w-full shrink-0 sm:w-auto">
              <Button
                variant="outline"
                className="h-10 w-full rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white px-4 shadow-none hover:bg-slate-50 sm:w-auto"
                asChild
              >
                <Link
                  to="/data-kk/$id"
                  params={{
                    id: String(anggota.kkId),
                  }}
                  preload="intent"
                >
                  <UsersRound className="size-4" />
                  Buka Kartu Keluarga
                </Link>
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* DATA DASAR */}
        <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-2">
          <Card className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
            <CardHeader className="border-b border-slate-200 px-5 py-4 sm:px-6">
              <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                <div className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
                  <UserRound className="size-4" />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <CardTitle className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">
                    Identitas
                  </CardTitle>

                  <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
                    Informasi pribadi anggota
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </CardHeader>

            <CardContent className="p-5 sm:p-6">
              <div className="grid gap-x-6 gap-y-5 sm:grid-cols-2">
                <Info
                  label="Nama Lengkap"
                  value={anggota.nama}
                  className="sm:col-span-2"
                />

                <Info label="NIK" value={anggota.nik} mono />

                <Info label="Jenis Kelamin" value={anggota.jenisKelamin} />

                <Info label="Tempat Lahir" value={anggota.tempatLahir} />

                <Info
                  label="Tanggal Lahir"
                  value={anggota.tanggalLahir}
                  icon={<CalendarDays className="size-3.5" />}
                />

                <Info label="Agama" value={anggota.agama} />

                <Info
                  label="Golongan Darah"
                  value={anggota.golonganDarah}
                  icon={<Droplets className="size-3.5" />}
                />

                <Info label="Pendidikan" value={anggota.pendidikan} />

                <Info
                  label="Pekerjaan"
                  value={anggota.pekerjaan}
                  icon={<BriefcaseBusiness className="size-3.5" />}
                />

                <Info
                  label="Nomor Anggota Dalam KK"
                  value={String(anggota.noUrut)}
                />
              </div>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>

          <Card className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
            <CardHeader className="border-b border-slate-200 px-5 py-4 sm:px-6">
              <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                <div className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
                  <MapPin className="size-4" />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <CardTitle className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">
                    Keluarga & Alamat
                  </CardTitle>

                  <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
                    Informasi keluarga dan domisili anggota
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </CardHeader>

            <CardContent className="p-5 sm:p-6">
              <div className="grid gap-x-6 gap-y-5 sm:grid-cols-2">
                <Info
                  label="Kepala Keluarga"
                  value={anggota.kepalaKeluarga}
                  className="sm:col-span-2"
                />

                <Info
                  label="Alamat"
                  value={anggota.alamat}
                  className="sm:col-span-2"
                />

                <Info
                  label="RT / RW"
                  value={formatRtRw(anggota.rt, anggota.rw)}
                />

                <Info label="Kode Pos" value={anggota.kodePos} />

                <Info label="Desa / Kelurahan" value={anggota.desaKelurahan} />

                <Info label="Kecamatan" value={anggota.kecamatan} />

                <Info label="Kabupaten / Kota" value={anggota.kabupatenKota} />

                <Info label="Provinsi" value={anggota.provinsi} />
              </div>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </div>

        {/* KESEHATAN */}
        <section className="mt-8">
          <div className="mb-4">
            <div className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-blue-600">
              Kesehatan
            </div>

            <h2 className="mt-1 text-xl font-semibold text-slate-900">
              Informasi Kesehatan
            </h2>

            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
              Ringkasan, perkembangan, dan riwayat pemeriksaan kesehatan
              anggota.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-6">
            <RingkasanKesehatan anggotaId={anggota.id} />

            <TrenKesehatan anggotaId={anggota.id} />

            <section className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm sm:p-6">
              <RiwayatPemeriksaan anggotaId={anggota.id} refreshKey={0} />
            </section>
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* META */}
        <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col gap-1 border-t border-slate-200 pt-4 text-xs text-slate-400 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
          <span>Data Anggota #{anggota.id}</span>

          <span>Terdaftar pada KK #{anggota.kkId}</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </main>
  )
}

type InfoProps = {
  label: string
  value: string | null
  icon?: ReactNode
  mono?: boolean
  className?: string
}

function Info({ label, value, icon, mono = false, className = '' }: InfoProps) {
  return (
    <div className={className}>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-400">
        {icon}
        {label}
      </div>

      <div
        className={[
          'mt-1 text-sm font-medium leading-6 text-slate-800',
          mono ? 'font-mono tabular-nums' : '',
        ].join(' ')}
      >
        {value || '-'}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function formatRtRw(rt: string | null, rw: string | null) {
  if (!rt && !rw) {
    return '-'
  }

  return `${rt ?? '-'} / ${rw ?? '-'}`
}

import { createFileRoute, Link } from '@tanstack/react-router'
import {
  ContactRound,
  Database,
  FileJson2,
  Files,
  LayoutList,
  UserRound,
} from 'lucide-react'

import { anggotaColumns } from '@/components/anggota/list/anggota-columns'
import {
  anggotaColumnLabels,
  anggotaSearchOptions,
} from '@/components/anggota/list/config'

import { DataCountBadge } from '@/components/data-list/data-count-badge'
import { DataListCard } from '@/components/data-list/data-list-card'
import { DataListEmpty } from '@/components/data-list/data-list-empty'
import { DataListHero } from '@/components/data-list/data-list-hero'
import { DataListLoading } from '@/components/data-list/data-list-loading'
import { DataListPage } from '@/components/data-list/data-list-page'
import { DataListStats } from '@/components/data-list/data-list-stats'

import { DataTable } from '@/components/data-table/data-table'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'

import { useListController } from '@/hooks/use-list-controller'

import { createListSearchValidator } from '@/lib/list-search'

import type { ListSearch } from '@/lib/list-search'

import { listAnggota } from '@/server/anggota'

const searchBy = ['all', 'nama', 'nik', 'alamat'] as const

const sortBy = ['nama', 'kepalaKeluarga', 'alamat', 'createdAt'] as const

type SearchBy = (typeof searchBy)[number]

type SortBy = (typeof sortBy)[number]

type DataAnggotaSearch = ListSearch<SearchBy, SortBy>

const allowedSorts = new Set<string>(sortBy)

const validateSearch = createListSearchValidator({
  searchBy,
  sortBy,
})

export const Route = createFileRoute('/data-anggota')({
  validateSearch,

  loaderDeps: ({ search }) => ({
    page: search.page ?? 1,

    limit: search.limit ?? 20,

    q: search.q ?? '',

    searchBy: search.by ?? 'all',

    sortBy: search.sort ?? 'nama',

    sortOrder: search.order ?? 'asc',
  }),

  loader: ({ deps }) =>
    listAnggota({
      data: {
        page: deps.page,

        limit: deps.limit,

        q: deps.q,

        searchBy: deps.searchBy,

        sortBy: deps.sortBy,

        sortOrder: deps.sortOrder,
      },
    }),

  pendingComponent: DataAnggotaPending,

  component: DataAnggotaPage,
})

function DataAnggotaPage() {
  const result = Route.useLoaderData()

  const search = Route.useSearch()

  const navigate = Route.useNavigate()

  const list = useListController<DataAnggotaSearch>({
    search,

    defaultSort: 'nama',

    defaultOrder: 'asc',

    allowedSorts,

    navigate,
  })

  const hasData = result.data.length > 0

  const isEmpty = !hasData && !search.q

  return (
    <DataListPage>
      <DataListHero
        icon={<ContactRound className="size-5" />}
        eyebrow="Database Jemaat"
        title="Data Anggota"
        description="Lihat dan kelola seluruh anggota jemaat beserta identitas, keluarga, alamat, dan informasi terkait."
        badge={
          <DataCountBadge>
            {result.pagination.total > 0
              ? `${result.pagination.total.toLocaleString(
                  'id-ID',
                )} anggota tersimpan`
              : 'Belum ada anggota tersimpan'}
          </DataCountBadge>
        }
        actions={
          <>
            <Button
              variant="outline"
              className="h-10 w-full rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white px-4 text-slate-700 shadow-sm hover:bg-slate-50 sm:w-auto"
              asChild
            >
              <Link to="/data-kk">
                <Database className="size-4 text-blue-600" />
                Data KK
              </Link>
            </Button>

            <Button
              className="h-10 w-full rounded-lg px-4 shadow-sm sm:w-auto"
              asChild
            >
              <Link to="/fitur/parser-kk">
                <FileJson2 className="size-4" />
                Tambah Data
              </Link>
            </Button>
          </>
        }
      />

      {hasData && (
        <DataListStats
          items={[
            {
              label: 'Total Anggota',

              value: result.pagination.total.toLocaleString('id-ID'),

              icon: <UserRound className="size-4" />,
            },

            {
              label: 'Ditampilkan',

              value: result.data.length.toLocaleString('id-ID'),

              icon: <LayoutList className="size-4" />,
            },

            {
              label: 'Halaman',

              value: `${result.pagination.page} / ${Math.max(
                result.pagination.totalPages,
                1,
              )}`,

              icon: <Files className="size-4" />,
            },
          ]}
        />
      )}

      {isEmpty ? (
        <DataListEmpty
          icon={<ContactRound className="size-7" />}
          title="Belum ada data anggota"
          description="Data anggota akan tersedia setelah Kartu Keluarga ditambahkan ke database."
          action={
            <Button asChild>
              <Link to="/fitur/parser-kk">
                <FileJson2 className="size-4" />
                Tambah Data KK
              </Link>
            </Button>
          }
        />
      ) : (
        <DataListCard
          pagination={result.pagination}
          onPageChange={list.pageChange}
          onPageSizeChange={list.pageSizeChange}
        >
          <DataTable
            columns={anggotaColumns}
            data={result.data}
            searchValue={search.q ?? ''}
            searchMode={search.by ?? 'all'}
            searchOptions={anggotaSearchOptions}
            columnLabels={anggotaColumnLabels}
            sorting={list.sorting}
            onSearchChange={list.searchChange}
            onSearchModeChange={list.searchByChange}
            onSortingChange={list.sortingChange}
          />
        </DataListCard>
      )}
    </DataListPage>
  )
}

function DataAnggotaPending() {
  return (
    <DataListPage>
      <DataListLoading />
    </DataListPage>
  )
}


############################################################
FILE: src/routes/data-kk_.$id.tsx
############################################################

import { createFileRoute, useRouter } from '@tanstack/react-router'

import { ShieldCheck, TriangleAlert } from 'lucide-react'

import { useState } from 'react'

import { EditKKDialog } from '@/components/kk/edit-kk-dialog'
import { KKDetailHeader } from '@/components/kk/detail/kk-detail-header'
import { KKInfoCard } from '@/components/kk/detail/kk-info-card'
import { KKMembersCard } from '@/components/kk/detail/kk-members-card'

import { useSensitiveKK } from '@/hooks/use-sensitive-kk'

import { getKKDetail } from '@/server/kk.functions'

export const Route = createFileRoute('/data-kk_/$id')({
  loader: async ({ params }) => {
    const id = Number(params.id)

    if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) {
      throw new Error('ID tidak valid.')
    }

    return getKKDetail({
      data: {
        id,
      },
    })
  },

  component: KKDetailPage,
})

function KKDetailPage() {
  const data = Route.useLoaderData()

  const router = useRouter()

  const [editOpen, setEditOpen] = useState(false)

  const sensitive = useSensitiveKK(data.id)

  async function handleSaved() {
    sensitive.hide()

    await router.invalidate()
  }

  return (
    <main className="bg-slate-50/70">
      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl px-4 py-7 sm:px-6 sm:py-8 lg:px-8 lg:py-10">
        <KKDetailHeader
          kepalaKeluarga={data.kepalaKeluarga}
          desaKelurahan={data.desaKelurahan}
          kecamatan={data.kecamatan}
          jumlahAnggota={data.anggota.length}
          sensitiveVisible={sensitive.isVisible}
          sensitiveLoading={sensitive.isLoading}
          onEdit={() => setEditOpen(true)}
          onToggleSensitive={sensitive.toggle}
        />

        {sensitive.error && (
          <div className="mb-6 flex items-start gap-3 rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50/60 px-4 py-3.5 text-sm text-red-700">
            <TriangleAlert className="mt-0.5 size-4 shrink-0" />

            <div className="min-w-0">
              <div className="font-medium">
                Data sensitif tidak dapat dibuka
              </div>

              <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs leading-5 text-red-600">
                {sensitive.error}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="space-y-6">
          <KKInfoCard
            noKK={sensitive.getNoKK(data.noKK)}
            kepalaKeluarga={data.kepalaKeluarga}
            alamat={data.alamat}
            rt={data.rt}
            rw={data.rw}
            desaKelurahan={data.desaKelurahan}
            kecamatan={data.kecamatan}
            kabupatenKota={data.kabupatenKota}
            kodePos={data.kodePos}
            provinsi={data.provinsi}
          />

          <KKMembersCard
            members={data.anggota}
            sensitiveVisible={sensitive.isVisible}
            getNik={sensitive.getNik}
          />
        </div>

        <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col gap-2 border-t border-slate-200 pt-4 text-xs text-slate-400 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
          <span>Data ID #{data.id}</span>

          <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <ShieldCheck className="size-3.5" />
            Data sensitif disimpan dalam keadaan terenkripsi
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <EditKKDialog
        id={data.id}
        open={editOpen}
        onOpenChange={setEditOpen}
        onSaved={handleSaved}
      />
    </main>
  )
}

import { createFileRoute, Link } from '@tanstack/react-router'
import {

  UsersRound,
} from 'lucide-react'

import { DataListStats } from '@/components/data-list/data-list-stats'

import { DataTable } from '@/components/data-table/data-table'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'

import { kkColumns } from '@/components/kk/list/kk-columns'
import { kkColumnLabels, kkSearchOptions } from '@/components/kk/list/config'

import { useListController } from '@/hooks/use-list-controller'

import { createListSearchValidator } from '@/lib/list-search'

import type { ListSearch } from '@/lib/list-search'

import { listKK } from '@/server/kk.functions'

const searchBy = ['all', 'nama', 'nik', 'alamat'] as const

const sortBy = [
  'kepalaKeluarga',
  'alamat',
  'jumlahAnggota',
  'createdAt',
] as const

type SearchBy = (typeof searchBy)[number]

type SortBy = (typeof sortBy)[number]

type DataKKSearch = ListSearch<SearchBy, SortBy>

const allowedSorts = new Set<string>(sortBy)

const validateSearch = createListSearchValidator({
  searchBy,
  sortBy,
})

export const Route = createFileRoute('/data-kk')({
  validateSearch,

  loaderDeps: ({ search }) => ({
    page: search.page ?? 1,

    limit: search.limit ?? 20,

    q: search.q ?? '',

    searchBy: search.by ?? 'all',

    sortBy: search.sort ?? 'createdAt',

    sortOrder: search.order ?? 'desc',
  }),

  loader: ({ deps }) =>
    listKK({
      data: {
        page: deps.page,

        limit: deps.limit,

        q: deps.q,

        searchBy: deps.searchBy,

        sortBy: deps.sortBy,

        sortOrder: deps.sortOrder,
      },
    }),

  pendingComponent: DataKKPending,

  component: DataKKPage,
})

function DataKKPage() {
  const result = Route.useLoaderData()

  const search = Route.useSearch()

  const navigate = Route.useNavigate()

  const list = useListController<DataKKSearch>({
    search,

    defaultSort: 'createdAt',

    defaultOrder: 'desc',

    allowedSorts,

    navigate,
  })

  const hasData = result.data.length > 0

  const isEmpty = !hasData && !search.q

        badge={
          <DataCountBadge>
            {result.pagination.total > 0
              ? `${result.pagination.total.toLocaleString(
                  'id-ID',
                )} Kartu Keluarga tersimpan`
              : 'Belum ada data tersimpan'}
          </DataCountBadge>
        }
        actions={
          <>
            <Button
              variant="outline"
              className="h-10 w-full rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white px-4 text-slate-700 shadow-sm hover:bg-slate-50 sm:w-auto"
              asChild
            >
              <Link to="/data-anggota">
                <UsersRound className="size-4 text-blue-600" />
                Data Anggota
              </Link>
            </Button>

            <Button
              className="h-10 w-full rounded-lg px-4 shadow-sm sm:w-auto"
              asChild
            >
              <Link to="/fitur/parser-kk">
                <FileJson2 className="size-4" />
                Tambah Data
              </Link>
            </Button>
          </>
        }
      />

      {hasData && (
        <DataListStats
          items={[
            {
              label: 'Total KK',

              value: result.pagination.total.toLocaleString('id-ID'),

              icon: <Database className="size-4" />,
            },

            {
              label: 'Ditampilkan',

              value: result.data.length.toLocaleString('id-ID'),

              icon: <LayoutList className="size-4" />,
            },

            {
              label: 'Halaman',

              value: `${result.pagination.page} / ${Math.max(
                result.pagination.totalPages,
                1,
              )}`,

              icon: <Files className="size-4" />,
            },
          ]}
        />
      )}

      {isEmpty ? (
        <DataListEmpty
          icon={<Users className="size-7" />}
          title="Belum ada data Kartu Keluarga"
          description="Tambahkan data Kartu Keluarga melalui Parser KK untuk mulai membangun database jemaat."
          action={
            <Button asChild>
              <Link to="/fitur/parser-kk">
                <FileJson2 className="size-4" />
                Tambah Data KK
              </Link>
            </Button>
          }
        />
      ) : (
        <DataListCard
          pagination={result.pagination}
          onPageChange={list.pageChange}
          onPageSizeChange={list.pageSizeChange}
        >
          <DataTable
            columns={kkColumns}
            data={result.data}
            searchValue={search.q ?? ''}
            searchMode={search.by ?? 'all'}
            searchOptions={kkSearchOptions}
            columnLabels={kkColumnLabels}
            sorting={list.sorting}
            onSearchChange={list.searchChange}
            onSearchModeChange={list.searchByChange}
            onSortingChange={list.sortingChange}
          />
        </DataListCard>
      )}
    </DataListPage>
  )
}

function DataKKPending() {
  return (
    <DataListPage>
      <DataListLoading />
    </DataListPage>
  )
}

import { createFileRoute, Link, useRouter } from '@tanstack/react-router'

import { ArrowLeft, LockKeyhole } from 'lucide-react'

import { useState } from 'react'

import { DaftarPemeriksaanEvent } from '@/components/kesehatan/daftar-pemeriksaan-event'
import { EventKesehatanHeader } from '@/components/kesehatan/event-kesehatan-header'
import { PetugasEvent } from '@/components/kesehatan/petugas-event'
import { WargaKesehatanSection } from '@/components/kesehatan/warga-kesehatan-section'

import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'

import { getEventKesehatan } from '@/server/kesehatan'

export const Route = createFileRoute('/event-kesehatan_/$id')({
  loader: async ({ params }) => {
    const id = Number(params.id)

    if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) {
      throw new Error('ID event tidak valid.')
    }

    return getEventKesehatan({
      data: {
        id,
      },
    })
  },

  component: EventKesehatanDetailPage,
})

function EventKesehatanDetailPage() {
  const event = Route.useLoaderData()

  const router = useRouter()

  const [refreshKey, setRefreshKey] = useState(0)

  async function handleChanged() {
    setRefreshKey((value) => value + 1)

    await router.invalidate()
  }

  return (
    <main className="bg-slate-50/70">
      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-6xl px-4 py-6 sm:px-6 sm:py-8 lg:px-8 lg:py-10">
        {/* BACK */}
        <div className="mb-4">
          <Button
            variant="ghost"
            size="sm"
            className="-ml-2 h-9 rounded-lg px-2 text-slate-500 hover:bg-white hover:text-slate-900"
            asChild
          >
            <Link to="/event-kesehatan">
              <ArrowLeft className="size-4" />
              Kembali ke Event
            </Link>
          </Button>
        </div>

        {/* HEADER */}
        <EventKesehatanHeader event={event} onChanged={handleChanged} />

        {/* CLOSED NOTICE */}
        {event.isClosed && (
          <div className="mb-6 flex items-start gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-4 py-4 shadow-sm sm:px-5">
            <div className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-slate-100 text-slate-500">
              <LockKeyhole className="size-4" />
            </div>

            <div className="min-w-0">
              <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">
                Event telah selesai
              </div>

              <p className="mt-1 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-500">
                Data pemeriksaan tetap dapat dilihat, tetapi event yang sudah
                ditutup tidak dapat ditambah atau diubah.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* EVENT AKTIF */}
        {!event.isClosed && (
          <div className="space-y-6">
            <PetugasEvent
              eventId={event.id}
              petugas={event.petugas}
              onChanged={handleChanged}
            />

            <WargaKesehatanSection
              eventId={event.id}
              petugas={event.petugas}
              refreshKey={refreshKey}
              onChanged={handleChanged}
            />
          </div>
        )}

        {/* DAFTAR PEMERIKSAAN */}
        <div className={event.isClosed ? '' : 'mt-6'}>
          <DaftarPemeriksaanEvent
            eventId={event.id}
            refreshKey={refreshKey}
            readOnly={event.isClosed}
            onChanged={handleChanged}
          />
        </div>
      </div>
    </main>
  )
}


############################################################
FILE: src/routes/event-kesehatan.tsx
############################################################

import { createFileRoute, useRouter } from '@tanstack/react-router'

import { CalendarHeart, ClipboardList, SearchX } from 'lucide-react'

import { CreateEventForm } from '@/components/kesehatan/create-event-form'
import { EventListToolbar } from '@/components/kesehatan/event-list-toolbar'

import type { EventStatus } from '@/components/kesehatan/event-list-toolbar'

import { EventList } from '@/components/kesehatan/event-list'

import { DataCountBadge } from '@/components/data-list/data-count-badge'
import { DataListHero } from '@/components/data-list/data-list-hero'
import { DataListPage } from '@/components/data-list/data-list-page'
import { DataPagination } from '@/components/data-list/data-pagination'

import type { PageSize } from '@/components/data-list/data-pagination'

import { listEventKesehatan } from '@/server/kesehatan'

type EventKesehatanSearch = {
  page?: number

  limit?: PageSize

  q?: string

  status?: Exclude<EventStatus, 'all'>

  tahun?: number
}

function validateSearch(search: Record<string, unknown>): EventKesehatanSearch {
  const result: EventKesehatanSearch = {}

  const page = Number(search.page)

  if (Number.isInteger(page) && page > 1) {
    result.page = page
  }

  const limit = Number(search.limit)

  if (limit === 10 || limit === 20 || limit === 50 || limit === 100) {
    result.limit = limit
  }

  if (typeof search.q === 'string') {
    const q = search.q.trim()

    if (q) {
      result.q = q
    }
  }

  if (search.status === 'aktif' || search.status === 'selesai') {
    result.status = search.status
  }

  const tahun = Number(search.tahun)

  if (Number.isInteger(tahun) && tahun >= 2000 && tahun <= 2100) {
    result.tahun = tahun
  }

  return result
}

export const Route = createFileRoute('/event-kesehatan')({
  validateSearch,

  loaderDeps: ({
    search,
  }): {
    page: number
    limit: PageSize
    q: string
    status: EventStatus
    tahun?: number
  } => ({
    page: search.page ?? 1,

    limit: search.limit ?? 10,

    q: search.q ?? '',

    status: search.status ?? 'all',

    tahun: search.tahun,
  }),

  loader: ({ deps }) =>
    listEventKesehatan({
      data: {
        page: deps.page,

        limit: deps.limit,

        q: deps.q,

        status: deps.status,

        tahun: deps.tahun,
      },
    }),

  component: EventKesehatanPage,
})

function EventKesehatanPage() {
  const result = Route.useLoaderData()

  const search = Route.useSearch()

  const navigate = Route.useNavigate()

  const router = useRouter()

  const events = result.data

  const status: EventStatus = search.status ?? 'all'

  const hasFilter = Boolean(search.q || search.status || search.tahun)

  function handleSearchChange(q: string) {
    void navigate({
      search: (previous) => ({
        ...previous,

        page: undefined,

        q: q.trim() || undefined,
      }),
    })
  }

  function handleStatusChange(nextStatus: EventStatus) {
    void navigate({
      search: (previous) => ({
        ...previous,

        page: undefined,

        status: nextStatus === 'all' ? undefined : nextStatus,
      }),
    })
  }

  function handleYearChange(tahun: number | undefined) {
    void navigate({
      search: (previous) => ({
        ...previous,

        page: undefined,

        tahun,
      }),
    })
  }

  function handleReset() {
    void navigate({
      search: (previous) => ({
        ...previous,

        page: undefined,

        q: undefined,

        status: undefined,

        tahun: undefined,
      }),
    })
  }

  function handlePageChange(page: number) {
    void navigate({
      search: (previous) => ({
        ...previous,

        page: page <= 1 ? undefined : page,
      }),
    })
  }

  function handlePageSizeChange(limit: PageSize) {
    void navigate({
      search: (previous) => ({
        ...previous,

        page: undefined,

        limit: limit === 10 ? undefined : limit,
      }),
    })
  }

  return (
    <DataListPage>
      <DataListHero
        icon={<CalendarHeart className="size-5" />}
        eyebrow="Kesehatan Jemaat"
        title="Event Kesehatan"
        description="Kelola kegiatan pemeriksaan kesehatan warga, mulai dari pembuatan event hingga pencatatan hasil pemeriksaan."
        badge={
          <DataCountBadge>
            {hasFilter
              ? `${result.pagination.total.toLocaleString(
                  'id-ID',
                )} event ditemukan`
              : result.pagination.total > 0
                ? `${result.pagination.total.toLocaleString(
                    'id-ID',
                  )} event tersimpan`
                : 'Belum ada event'}
          </DataCountBadge>
        }
      />

      <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[360px_minmax(0,1fr)]">
        <aside className="min-w-0 lg:sticky lg:top-28 lg:self-start">
          <CreateEventForm
            onCreated={async () => {
              await router.invalidate()
            }}
          />
        </aside>

        <section className="min-w-0 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
          <div className="border-b border-slate-200 px-4 py-4 sm:px-5">
            <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
              <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-3">
                <div className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
                  <ClipboardList className="size-4" />
                </div>

                <div className="min-w-0">
                  <h2 className="font-semibold text-slate-900">Daftar Event</h2>

                  <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500">
                    {result.pagination.total > 0
                      ? `${result.pagination.total.toLocaleString(
                          'id-ID',
                        )} event sesuai filter`
                      : 'Tidak ada event yang sesuai'}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <EventListToolbar
              searchValue={search.q ?? ''}
              status={status}
              tahun={search.tahun}
              onSearchChange={handleSearchChange}
              onStatusChange={handleStatusChange}
              onYearChange={handleYearChange}
              onReset={handleReset}
            />
          </div>

          <div className="p-4 sm:p-5">
            {events.length > 0 ? (
              <EventList events={events} />
            ) : (
              <div className="flex min-h-64 flex-col items-center justify-center px-6 text-center">
                <div className="flex size-12 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-slate-100 text-slate-400">
                  <SearchX className="size-5" />
                </div>

                <h3 className="mt-4 font-semibold text-slate-800">
                  {hasFilter ? 'Event tidak ditemukan' : 'Belum ada event'}
                </h3>

                <p className="mt-1 max-w-sm text-sm leading-6 text-slate-500">
                  {hasFilter
                    ? 'Coba ubah kata pencarian, status, atau tahun event.'
                    : 'Event kesehatan yang dibuat akan muncul di sini.'}
                </p>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {result.pagination.total > 0 && (
            <div className="border-t border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/50 px-4 py-4 sm:px-5">
              <DataPagination
                pagination={result.pagination}
                onPageChange={handlePageChange}
                onPageSizeChange={handlePageSizeChange}
              />
            </div>
          )}
        </section>
      </div>
    </DataListPage>
  )
}

import { createFileRoute } from '@tanstack/react-router'
import { useState } from 'react'

import { CreateKKDialog } from '@/components/kk/create/create-kk-dialog'
import { KKParserHeader } from '@/components/kk/parser/kk-parser-header'
import { ParserInputCard } from '@/components/kk/parser/parser-input-card'
import { ParserPreviewCard } from '@/components/kk/parser/parser-preview-card'
import { ParserSaveCard } from '@/components/kk/parser/parser-save-card'
import { ParserValidationCard } from '@/components/kk/parser/parser-validation-card'
import { PrivacyNotice } from '@/components/kk/parser/privacy-notice'

import { useKKParser } from '@/hooks/use-kk-parser'

export const Route = createFileRoute('/fitur/parser-kk')({
  component: ParserKKPage,
})

function ParserKKPage() {
  const parser = useKKParser()

  const [createOpen, setCreateOpen] = useState(false)

  return (
    <main className="bg-slate-50/70">
      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl px-4 py-7 sm:px-6 sm:py-8 lg:px-8 lg:py-10">
        {/* HEADER */}
        <KKParserHeader onAddManual={() => setCreateOpen(true)} />

        {/* PRIVACY */}
        <div className="mb-6">
          <PrivacyNotice />
        </div>

        {/* PARSER WORKSPACE */}
        <section className="grid min-w-0 gap-6 lg:grid-cols-2 lg:items-stretch">
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <ParserInputCard
              text={parser.text}
              error={parser.parseError}
              canClear={parser.canClear}
              onTextChange={parser.setText}
              onParse={parser.handleParse}
              onClear={parser.handleClear}
            />
          </div>

          <div className="min-w-0">
            <ParserPreviewCard
              data={parser.previewData}
              isValid={parser.isValid}
              showSensitive={parser.showSensitive}
              onToggleSensitive={parser.toggleSensitive}
              onCopy={parser.handleCopy}
            />
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* RESULT */}
        {parser.result && (
          <section className="mt-6 space-y-6">
            <ParserValidationCard
              isValid={parser.isValid}
              errors={parser.validationErrors}
            />

            <ParserSaveCard
              canSave={parser.canSave}
              isSaving={parser.isSaving}
              isSaved={parser.isSaved}
              onSave={parser.handleSave}
            />
          </section>
        )}
      </div>

      <CreateKKDialog
        open={createOpen}
        onOpenChange={setCreateOpen}
        onCreated={() => {
          setCreateOpen(false)
        }}
      />
    </main>
  )
}

type StatCardProps = {
  icon: ReactNode
  label: string
  value: number
  description: string
  to: '/data-kk' | '/data-anggota' | '/event-kesehatan'
}

function StatCard({ icon, label, value, description, to }: StatCardProps) {
  return (
    <Link
      to={to}
      preload="intent"
      className="group rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm transition-[border-color,box-shadow,background-color] duration-200 ease-out hover:border-blue-200 hover:bg-blue-50/10 hover:shadow-md"
    >
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex size-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
          {icon}
        </div>

        <ArrowRight className="size-4 text-slate-300 transition-colors duration-200 ease-out group-hover:text-blue-500" />
      </div>

      <div className="mt-5 text-2xl font-bold tabular-nums tracking-tight text-slate-900">
        {value.toLocaleString('id-ID')}
      </div>

      <div className="mt-1 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">{label}</div>

      <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-400">{description}</div>
    </Link>
  )
}

type MenuCardProps = {
  icon: ReactNode
  title: string
  description: string

  to: '/data-kk' | '/data-anggota' | '/event-kesehatan' | '/fitur/parser-kk'
}

function MenuCard({ icon, title, description, to }: MenuCardProps) {
  return (
    <Link
      to={to}
      preload="intent"
      className="group flex min-h-52 flex-col rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm transition-[border-color,box-shadow,background-color] duration-200 ease-out hover:border-blue-200 hover:bg-blue-50/10 hover:shadow-md"
    >
      <div className="flex size-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600 transition-colors duration-200 ease-out group-hover:bg-blue-100">
        {icon}
      </div>

      <div className="mt-4 text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">{title}</div>

      <p className="mt-2 flex-1 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-500">
        {description}
      </p>

      <div className="mt-4 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm font-medium text-blue-600">
        Buka menu
        <ArrowRight className="size-4 transition-colors duration-200 ease-out" />
      </div>
    </Link>
  )
}

function WorkflowStep({
  number,
  title,
  description,
}: {
  number: string
  title: string
  description: string
}) {
  return (
    <div className="p-5 sm:p-6">
      <div className="text-xs font-bold tracking-[0.14em] text-blue-600">
        {number}
      </div>

      <div className="mt-2 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">{title}</div>

      <p className="mt-1.5 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">{description}</p>
    </div>
  )
}

function formatTanggal(value: string) {
  const [year, month, day] = value.split('-').map(Number)

  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('id-ID', {
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'long',
    year: 'numeric',
  }).format(new Date(year, month - 1, day))
}

import { createServerFn } from '@tanstack/react-start'

import { db } from '../db.server'
import { maskIdentityNumber } from '../kk/utils.server'
import { decryptValue } from '../security/encrypt.server'

import { anggotaIdSchema } from './schemas'

export const getAnggotaDetail = createServerFn({
  method: 'GET',
})
  .validator(anggotaIdSchema)
  .handler(async ({ data }) => {
    const anggota = await db.anggotaKeluarga.findUnique({
      where: {
        id: data.id,
      },

      select: {
        id: true,
        kkId: true,
        noUrut: true,

        nama: true,

        nikCiphertext: true,
        nikIv: true,
        nikAuthTag: true,

        jenisKelamin: true,
        tempatLahir: true,
        tanggalLahir: true,

        agama: true,
        pendidikan: true,
        pekerjaan: true,
        golonganDarah: true,

        kk: {
          select: {
            kepalaKeluarga: true,

            alamat: true,
            rt: true,
            rw: true,

            desaKelurahan: true,
            kecamatan: true,
            kabupatenKota: true,
            kodePos: true,
            provinsi: true,
          },
        },
      },
    })

    if (!anggota) {
      throw new Error('Data anggota tidak ditemukan.')
    }

    const nik = decryptValue({
      ciphertext: anggota.nikCiphertext,
      iv: anggota.nikIv,
      authTag: anggota.nikAuthTag,
    })

    return {
      id: anggota.id,
      kkId: anggota.kkId,
      noUrut: anggota.noUrut,

      nama: anggota.nama,
      nik: maskIdentityNumber(nik),

      jenisKelamin: anggota.jenisKelamin,

      tempatLahir: anggota.tempatLahir,
      tanggalLahir: anggota.tanggalLahir,

      agama: anggota.agama,
      pendidikan: anggota.pendidikan,
      pekerjaan: anggota.pekerjaan,
      golonganDarah: anggota.golonganDarah,

      kepalaKeluarga: anggota.kk.kepalaKeluarga,

      alamat: anggota.kk.alamat,
      rt: anggota.kk.rt,
      rw: anggota.kk.rw,

      desaKelurahan: anggota.kk.desaKelurahan,
      kecamatan: anggota.kk.kecamatan,
      kabupatenKota: anggota.kk.kabupatenKota,
      kodePos: anggota.kk.kodePos,
      provinsi: anggota.kk.provinsi,
    }
  })
